  207:	/*============================================================================
  208:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_core_initialize
  209:	** Visibility:       Menu core
  210:	** Description:      This is the initialization function for the Menu Core This
  211:	**                   function initializes all status variable maintained by
  212:	**                   Menu core - chart and interface.
  213:	** Invocation:       Invoked by application.
  214:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_init_type_U8 indicates the init type as Cold or Warm.
  215:	** Critical Section: None.
  216:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
  217:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
  218:	**==========================================================================*/
  219:	void hmi_menu_initialize(void)
  220:	{
  221:	  UINT8 i;
  222:	#ifndef MATLAB_MEX_FILE
  223:	  hmi_menu_core_01_model_initializer();
  224:	#endif
  225:	  for(i=0; i<NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE ; i++)
  226:	  {
  227:	#ifndef MATLAB_MEX_FILE
  228:	    (void)initialize_hmi_menu_core_01_chart(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[i]);
  229:	#endif
  230:	    l_menu_instance_array[i].inst_status_BOOL = HMI_MENU_LOGIC_INST_INACTIVE;
  231:	    l_menu_instance_array[i].timer_status_BOOL = FALSE;
  232:	  }
  233:	
  234:	  Current_focus_menu_ptr = FNULL;
  235:	  Current_focus_menu_inst_no_U8 = NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE;
  236:	  l_nb_of_active_instance_S8          = -1;
  237:	  l_end_of_fast_scroll_BOOL    = FALSE;
  238:	
  239:	#if defined(LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED) && defined(HMI_MENU_SMOOTH_FAST_ANIMATION)
  240:	    l_screen_id_in_queue =  LSH_NUMBER_OF_LOGIC_STATES;/* Invalid screen id*/
  241:	#endif
  242:	}
  243:	/*============================================================================
  244:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_rx_button_handler
  245:	** Visibility:       global
  246:	** Description:      The function handles the buttons posted on Menu.
  247:	**                   It does four functionalities
  248:	**                   1.Checks the Button Input Nature.
  249:	**                   2.Informs the specific component whether to process the
  250:	**                     Button in the Menu or not.
  251:	**                   3.If to be processed then informs the specific action
  252:	**                     to the logic (menu_chart)function.
  253:	**                   4.Returns the status of the Button Action.
  254:	** Invocation:       By the LSH
  255:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_button_command_U16 - Button ID and State
  256:	                     UINT8 fl_hmi_menu_core_btn_hndlr_ret - LSH_STATUS_ABSORBED
  257:	                                                            LSH_STATUS_ACTION
  258:	                                                            LSH_STATUS_NONE
  259:	** Critical Section: None.
  260:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
  261:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
  262:	**==========================================================================*/

  263:	UINT8 hmi_menu_rx_button_handler(LSH_EVENT_HANDLER_STATUS_T *p_event_hndl_status_SP)

  303:	/*============================================================================
  304:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_is_menu_action_allowed_in_anim_state
  305:	** Visibility:       global
  306:	** Description:      This function return whether menu action is allowed in anim stae
  307:	** Invocation:       By Menu core chart
  308:	** Inputs/Outputs:   fl_menu_action_U8
  309:	** Critical Section: None.
  310:	** Created:          27-June-2013 by csakthiv
  311:	** Updated:          27-June-2013 by csakthiv
  312:	**==========================================================================*/
  313:	BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_is_menu_action_allowed_in_anim_state(UINT8 fl_menu_action_U8)
  314:	{
  315:	   UINT8 i;
  316:	   BOOLEAN fl_menu_action_allowed_Bool = FALSE;
  317:	   for(i = 0; i < HMI_MENU_ACTION_IN_ANIM_COUNT; i++)
  318:	   {
  319:	      if(hmi_menu_actions_allowed_in_anim[i] == fl_menu_action_U8)
  320:	      {
  321:	         fl_menu_action_allowed_Bool = TRUE;
  322:	         break;
  323:	      }
  324:	   }
  325:	   return fl_menu_action_allowed_Bool;
  326:	}
  327:	/*============================================================================
  328:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_button_processing
  329:	** Visibility:       global
  330:	** Description:      This function is used to process the Presentation.
  331:	**                   It does below functionalities
  332:	**                   1.Maps the Screen ID to the Menu ID.
  333:	**                   2.Checks the Menu instance w.r.t Menu ID and finds the
  334:	**                     Instance.
  335:	**                   3.Routes to the corresponding presentation Call backs.
  336:	** Invocation:       By the LSH
  337:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_button_command_U16 - Button ID and State
  338:	                     UINT8 fl_hmi_menu_core_btn_hndlr_ret - LSH_STATUS_ABSORBED
  339:	                                                            LSH_STATUS_ACTION
  340:	                                                            LSH_STATUS_NONE
  341:	** Critical Section: None.
  342:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
  343:	** Updated:          16-Apr-2010 by jpradeep
  344:	**==========================================================================*/
  345:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_button_processing(MENU_CONTROL_T *fl_current_active_menu_SP,UINT16 p_button_command_U16)
  346:	{
  347:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T                 fl_cb_result_e;
  348:	    UINT8                            fl_menu_btn_action_U8;
  349:	    BOOLEAN                          fl_timer_status_BOOL;
  350:	    UINT8                            i;
  351:	    UINT8                            fl_table_index;
  352:	    UINT8                            fl_total_btn_acns_mapped;
  353:	    UINT8                            fl_menu_inst_idx_U8;
  354:	    MENU_BTN_TO_ACTIONS_MAP_T const *fl_btn_action_map_SP;
  355:	
  356:	    fl_cb_result_e        = MENU_BTN_PROCESS_MENU;
  357:	
  358:	    if(fl_current_active_menu_SP != FNULL)
  359:	    {
  360:	        fl_menu_inst_idx_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id);
  361:	
  362:	        if(fl_menu_inst_idx_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
  363:	        {
  364:	            fl_timer_status_BOOL = hmi_menu_logic_is_open_timer_enabled(fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id);
  365:	            /* Button Callback called to either process the button by Menu or not*/
  366:	            if(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id].cb != FNULL)
  367:	            {
  368:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id].cb(MENU_ACTION_VALIDATE_BUTTON, fl_current_active_menu_SP, p_button_command_U16);
  369:	            }
  370:	            /* Do not restart the timer when cb result is MENU_BTN_NONE or MENU_BTN_ABSORBED - UP375 CR*/
  371:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_NOT_RESTART_TIMER_ON_NONE_ABSORBED
  372:	            if((fl_timer_status_BOOL == TRUE) && ((fl_cb_result_e != MENU_BTN_NONE) && (fl_cb_result_e != MENU_BTN_ABSORBED)))
  373:	            {
  374:	                /* Restart the menu open timer for any button press and also internal fast scroll button event */
  375:	                /* Restart the timer only it is already running. If it is stopped it should not be restarted again*/
  376:	                hmi_menu_logic_start_timer(MENU_CORE_OPEN_TIMER,fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id);
  377:	            }
  378:	#else
  379:	      if(fl_timer_status_BOOL == TRUE)
  380:	            {
  381:	                /* Restart the menu open timer for any button press and also internal fast scroll button event */
  382:	                /* Restart the timer only it is already running. If it is stopped it should not be restarted again*/
  383:	                hmi_menu_logic_start_timer(MENU_CORE_OPEN_TIMER,fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id);
  384:	            }
  385:	#endif
  386:	            if((fl_cb_result_e == MENU_BTN_PROCESS_MENU) || (fl_cb_result_e == MENU_BTN_CASCADED))
  387:	            {
  388:	                /* The value is force to MENU_BTN_NONE if none of the btn action is mapped
  389:	                In case if it matches then the return value will be updated by hmi_menu_chart_Btn_trigger() */
  390:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_BTN_NONE;
  391:	
  392:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_FAST_SCROLL_ENABLE
  393:	                /* check made for processing the button if not in the Scrolling state 
  394:	                It is useful to not process the buttons when in the Fast scroll state
  395:	                Until the Fast scroll state is not complete, the other buttons should 
  396:	                not be processed. Supppose it is processed then the behaviour seems
  397:	                to be affected as the Menu chart logic works based on the btn events.   
  398:	                 Note:
  399:	                 The buttons blocked are sent by application for Menu core processing 
  400:	                 i.e MENU_BTN_PROCESS_MENU
  401:	                */
  402:	                if((FALSE != hmi_menu_chart_is_FS_st(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_menu_inst_idx_U8])) && (FALSE == hmi_menu_logic_FS_process_valid_Btn(p_button_command_U16)))
  403:	                {
  404:	                    fl_cb_result_e = MENU_BTN_ABSORBED;
  405:	                }
  406:	                else
  407:	#endif
  408:	                    {
  409:	                    /* Map the Button Actions to Menu Actions w.r.to menu mapped table */
  410:	                    fl_table_index = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id].table_index;
  411:	                    if(fl_table_index < HMI_MENU_TABLE_COUNT)
  412:	                    {
  413:	                        fl_btn_action_map_SP = hmi_menu_map_actions_table_ptr[fl_table_index].hmi_menu_map_actions_table;
  414:	                        fl_total_btn_acns_mapped = hmi_menu_map_actions_table_ptr[fl_table_index].hmi_menu_total_btn_acns_mapped;
  415:	                        
  416:	                        for(i=0; i < fl_total_btn_acns_mapped; i++)
  417:	                        {
  418:	                            if(fl_btn_action_map_SP[i].button_action == p_button_command_U16)
  419:	                            {
  420:	                                fl_menu_btn_action_U8 = fl_btn_action_map_SP[i].menu_action;
  421:	                                if(fl_menu_btn_action_U8 != MENU_ACTION_NONE)
  422:	                                {
  423:	                                    fl_cb_result_e = (MENU_CB_RESULT_T)hmi_menu_chart_Btn_trigger(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_menu_inst_idx_U8], fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id, fl_menu_btn_action_U8, fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_state);
  424:	                                }
  425:	                                else
  426:	                                {
  427:	                                    fl_cb_result_e = MENU_BTN_ABSORBED;
  428:	                                }
  429:	                                break;
  430:	                            }
  431:	                        }
  432:	                    }
  433:	                }
  434:	            }
  435:	            else if((fl_cb_result_e == MENU_BTN_RESTART_TIMER_ABSORBED) || (fl_cb_result_e == MENU_BTN_RESTART_TIMER_NONE))
  436:	            {
  437:	                hmi_menu_logic_start_timer(MENU_CORE_OPEN_TIMER,fl_current_active_menu_SP->menu_id);
  438:	                if(fl_cb_result_e == MENU_BTN_RESTART_TIMER_ABSORBED)
  439:	                {
  440:	                    fl_cb_result_e = MENU_BTN_ABSORBED;
  441:	                }
  442:	                else
  443:	                {
  444:	                    fl_cb_result_e = MENU_BTN_NONE;
  445:	                }
  446:	            }
  447:	            else
  448:	            {
  449:	                /* Do Nothing */
  450:	            }
  451:	        }
  452:	        else
  453:	        {
  454:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_BTN_NONE;
  455:	        }
  456:	    }
  457:	    else
  458:	    {
  459:	        fl_cb_result_e = MENU_BTN_NONE;
  460:	    }
  461:	
  462:	    return(fl_cb_result_e);
  463:	}
  464:	/*============================================================================

  469:	** Invocation:       By the logic

  591:	  else

 1012:	/*============================================================================
 1013:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_update_current_focus_menu_inst
 1014:	** Visibility:       global
 1015:	** Description:      Interface to update current menu focus ptr based on active instance
 1016:	** Invocation:       Menu core logic
 1017:	** Inputs:           None
 1018:	** Outputs:          Available menu instance.
 1019:	** Critical Section: None.
 1020:	** Created:          05-July-2013 by csakthiv
 1021:	** Updated:          
 1022:	**==========================================================================*/
 1023:	static void hmi_menu_logic_update_current_focus_menu_ptr(void)
 1024:	{
 1025:	   UINT8 fl_inst_U8;
 1026:	   UINT8 fl_current_focus_inst_U8 = 0;
 1027:	   UINT8 i;
 1028:	
 1029:	   if(l_nb_of_active_instance_S8 != -1)
 1030:	   {
 1031:	       for(i = 0; i < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE; i++)
 1032:	       {
 1033:	           if(l_menu_instance_array[i].inst_status_BOOL == HMI_MENU_LOGIC_INST_ACTIVE)
 1034:	           {
 1035:	               fl_inst_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(l_menu_instance_array[i].menu_id);
 1036:	               if(fl_inst_U8 >= fl_current_focus_inst_U8)
 1037:	               {
 1038:	                   fl_current_focus_inst_U8 =  fl_inst_U8;
 1039:	               }
 1040:	           }
 1041:	       }
 1042:	       Current_focus_menu_inst_no_U8 = fl_current_focus_inst_U8;
 1043:	       Current_focus_menu_ptr = &l_menu_instance_array[Current_focus_menu_inst_no_U8];
 1044:	   }
 1045:	   else
 1046:	   {
 1047:	       Current_focus_menu_inst_no_U8 = NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE;
 1048:	       Current_focus_menu_ptr = FNULL;
 1049:	   }
 1050:	   if(Current_focus_menu_ptr != FNULL)
 1051:	   {
 1052:	       hmi_menu_logic_start_timer(MENU_CORE_OPEN_TIMER, Current_focus_menu_ptr->menu_id);
 1053:	       hmi_menu_logic_enable_open_timer_status(Current_focus_menu_ptr->menu_id);
 1054:	   }
 1055:	
 1056:	}

 1082:	/*============================================================================
 1083:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_create_menu_instance
 1084:	** Visibility:       global
 1085:	** Description:      This function is used shall take the close action when
 1086:	**                   the Menu needs to be closed.
 1087:	** Invocation:       By the Application
 1088:	** Inputs:           p_close_menu_id_e - Menu ID
 1089:	** Outputs:          None
 1090:	** Critical Section: None.
 1091:	** Created:          6-Aug-2013 by csakthiv
 1092:	** Updated:          
 1093:	**==========================================================================*/
 1094:	static UINT8 hmi_menu_logic_create_menu_instance(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_open_menu_id_e)
 1095:	{
 1096:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_no_U8 = HMI_MENU_INST_INVALID;
 1097:	    MENU_CONTROL_T        *fl_menu_open_ptr;
 1098:	
 1099:	    if(l_nb_of_active_instance_S8 < (NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE-1))
 1100:	    {
 1101:	        fl_menu_inst_no_U8  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_open_menu_id_e);
 1102:	        if(fl_menu_inst_no_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 1103:	        {
 1104:	            fl_menu_open_ptr                     = hmi_menu_logic_get_required_menu_instance(fl_menu_inst_no_U8);
 1105:	            /* If the current instance is in active state close the menu and the create it
 1106:	            again with the new menu id. This is to do proper closing of current instance
 1107:	            timer and chart*/
 1108:	            if(fl_menu_open_ptr->inst_status_BOOL == HMI_MENU_LOGIC_INST_ACTIVE)
 1109:	            {
 1110:	              hmi_menu_logic_destroy_menu_instance(fl_menu_open_ptr->menu_id);
 1111:	            }
 1112:	            fl_menu_open_ptr->inst_status_BOOL = HMI_MENU_LOGIC_INST_ACTIVE;
 1113:	            l_nb_of_active_instance_S8++;
 1114:	         }
 1115:	    }
 1116:	    return fl_menu_inst_no_U8;
 1117:	}
 1118:	/*============================================================================
 1119:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_destroy_menu_instance
 1120:	** Visibility:       global
 1121:	** Description:      This function is used shall take the close action when
 1122:	**                   the Menu needs to be closed.
 1123:	** Invocation:       By the Application
 1124:	** Inputs:           p_close_menu_id_e - Menu ID
 1125:	** Outputs:          None
 1126:	** Critical Section: None.
 1127:	** Created:          6-Aug-2013 by csakthiv
 1128:	** Updated:          30-Jan-2014 by csakthiv
 1129:	**==========================================================================*/
 1130:	static void hmi_menu_logic_destroy_menu_instance(MENU_LIST_ID_T  fl_close_menu_id_e)
 1131:	{
 1132:	    MENU_CONTROL_T        *fl_menu_close_ptr;
 1133:	    UINT8                  fl_menu_inst_no_U8;
 1134:	
 1135:	    fl_menu_close_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(fl_close_menu_id_e);
 1136:	
 1137:	    if(fl_menu_close_ptr != FNULL)
 1138:	    {
 1139:	        fl_menu_inst_no_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_close_menu_id_e);
 1140:	        if(fl_menu_inst_no_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 1141:	        {
 1142:	            HMI_MENU_STOP_TIMER(fl_close_menu_id_e, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_no_U8].menu_open_timer_id);
 1143:	            #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1144:	            HMI_MENU_STOP_WAIT_TEMP_TIMER(fl_close_menu_id_e, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_no_U8].menu_wait_timer_id);
 1145:	            #endif
 1146:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_FAST_SCROLL_ENABLE
 1147:	            HMI_MENU_FS_STOP_TIMER(fl_close_menu_id_e, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_no_U8].menu_fastscroll_timer_id);
 1148:	      #endif
 1149:	            hmi_menu_chart_close(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_menu_inst_no_U8]);
 1150:	            fl_menu_close_ptr->inst_status_BOOL = HMI_MENU_LOGIC_INST_INACTIVE;
 1151:	            l_nb_of_active_instance_S8--;
 1152:	        }
 1153:	    }
 1154:	}
 1155:	
 1156:	
 1157:	/*============================================================================
 1158:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open
 1159:	** Visibility:       global
 1160:	** Description:      This function shall open the SubMenu.
 1161:	** Invocation:       By the Menu_logic.c
 1162:	** Inputs:           MENU_DEF_T   *p_open_submenu_ptr
 1163:	**                   UINT8       p_menu_inst_U8
 1164:	**                   MENU_ACTION_T p_menu_action_e
 1165:	** Outputs:          MENU_CB_RESULT_T
 1166:	** Critical Section: None.
 1167:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1168:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1169:	**==========================================================================*/
 1170:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(const MENU_DEF_T *p_open_submenu_ptr, const MENU_DEF_T *p_current_menu_ptr, UINT8 p_menu_inst_U8, MENU_ACTION_T p_menu_action_e)
 1171:	{
 1172:	  MENU_CB_RESULT_T        fl_cb_result_e;
 1173:	  MENU_CONTROL_T         *fl_cur_menu_ptr;
 1174:	  MENU_LIST_ID_T          fl_get_menu_index_e;
 1175:	
 1176:	  fl_cb_result_e         = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 1177:	
 1178:	  fl_get_menu_index_e    = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_index(p_open_submenu_ptr);
 1179:	  if(TOTAL_MENU_ID != fl_get_menu_index_e)
 1180:	  {
 1181:	      fl_cur_menu_ptr        = hmi_menu_logic_get_required_menu_instance(p_menu_inst_U8);
 1182:	      if(fl_cur_menu_ptr != FNULL)
 1183:	      {
 1184:	       /* Always submenu will be opened in the same menu instance 
 1185:	       and the parent menu and submenu screens should have the same priority*/
 1186:	       hmi_menu_fast_scroll_inactive(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[p_menu_inst_U8]);
 1187:	       hmi_menu_logic_create_menu(fl_get_menu_index_e, p_menu_inst_U8);
 1188:	         if (hmi_menu_logic_is_focus_entry_valid(fl_cur_menu_ptr) == FALSE)
 1189:	         {
 1190:	             /* if focus entry= 0, is greyout or focus disabled, then focus entry will incremented till valid FE*/
 1191:	             /* if none of the item is valid, then focus entry will be zero*/
 1192:	             hmi_menu_logic_down_focus_entry(fl_cur_menu_ptr);
 1193:	         }
 1194:	         /* when submenu can have different timeout than the parent menu,so restart the timer with submenus timeout value
 1195:	         * eventhough timer restarted on parenet timeout value on pressing right button.*/
 1196:	         hmi_menu_logic_start_timer(MENU_CORE_OPEN_TIMER,fl_cur_menu_ptr->menu_id);
 1197:	
 1198:	       if(((fl_cur_menu_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) == 0) &&
 1199:	         (p_menu_action_e != MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_CHILD))
 1200:	       {
 1201:	         hmi_menu_logic_load_menu_level(fl_cur_menu_ptr);
 1202:	       }
 1203:	       if(((fl_cur_menu_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) == 0) &&
 1204:	         (p_menu_action_e == MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_CHILD))
 1205:	       {
 1206:	         hmi_menu_logic_Set_ParentFocus(fl_cur_menu_ptr, p_current_menu_ptr);
 1207:	       }
 1208:	       if(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_cur_menu_ptr->menu_id].cb != FNULL)
 1209:	       {
 1210:	         fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_cur_menu_ptr->menu_id].cb(p_menu_action_e, fl_cur_menu_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 1211:	         #ifdef HMI_MENU_HISTORY_HANDLING_FEATURE
 1212:	         if((fl_cur_menu_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_HISTORY_MENU) != 0)
 1213:	         {
 1214:	           HMI_MENU_HISTORY_STORE_MENU_ID(fl_cur_menu_ptr->menu_id);
 1215:	         }
 1216:	         #endif
 1217:	       }
 1218:	       }
 1219:	  }
 1220:	  return(fl_cb_result_e);
 1221:	}
 1222:	/*============================================================================
 1223:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_index
 1224:	** Visibility:       global
 1225:	** Description:      This function is used to find the menu ID(index) in the
 1226:	**                   hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table to get loaded.
 1227:	** Invocation:       By the menu logic.c
 1228:	** Inputs:           MENU_CONTROL_T *fl_menu_ID_ptr
 1229:	**                   MENU_LIST_ID_T  menu_ID_index
 1230:	** Outputs:          Gives the  menu_ID_index.
 1231:	** Critical Section: None.
 1232:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1233:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1234:	**==========================================================================*/
 1235:	static MENU_LIST_ID_T hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_index(const MENU_DEF_T *fl_menu_ID_ptr)
 1236:	{
 1237:	  UINT8  i;
 1238:	  MENU_LIST_ID_T  fl_ret_menu_def_index_e;
 1239:	
 1240:	  fl_ret_menu_def_index_e = TOTAL_MENU_ID;
 1241:	  for(i=0; i<TOTAL_MENU_ID; i++)
 1242:	  {
 1243:	    if(fl_menu_ID_ptr == &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[i])
 1244:	    {
 1245:	      fl_ret_menu_def_index_e = (MENU_LIST_ID_T)i;
 1246:	      break;
 1247:	    }
 1248:	  }
 1249:	  return(fl_ret_menu_def_index_e);
 1250:	}
 1251:	/*============================================================================
 1252:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_create_menu
 1253:	** Visibility:       global
 1254:	** Description:      This function is used to load and create the Menu parameter
 1255:	**                   while opening the Menu.
 1256:	** Invocation:       By the Menu_interface.c
 1257:	** Inputs:           MENU_LIST_ID_T  p_menu_id_e
 1258:	**                   SINT8           p_menu_inst_val_S8
 1259:	** Outputs:          Points to the Menu Instance.
 1260:	** Critical Section: None.
 1261:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1262:	** Updated:          03-Mar-2014 by csakthiv
 1263:	**==========================================================================*/
 1264:	static void hmi_menu_logic_create_menu(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_e, UINT8 p_menu_inst_val_U8)
 1265:	{
 1266:	  MENU_CONTROL_T *fl_load_menu_ptr;
 1267:	
 1268:	  fl_load_menu_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_required_menu_instance(p_menu_inst_val_U8);
 1269:	  if(fl_load_menu_ptr != FNULL)
 1270:	  {
 1271:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->menu_id            = p_menu_id_e;
 1272:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->focus_entry_U16    = 0;
 1273:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->focus_line_U16     = 0;
 1274:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->line_fast_inc      = 1;
 1275:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->displayed_lines    = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_id_e].total_display_lines;
 1276:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->menu_entry_nbr     = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_id_e].total_entry_nbr;
 1277:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->menu_properties    = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_id_e].properties;
 1278:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->menu_state         = MENU_STATE_NORMAL;
 1279:	     fl_load_menu_ptr->selected_entry_U16 = MENU_INVALID_ENTRY;
 1280:	     l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[p_menu_inst_val_U8].l_menu_id_U8 = p_menu_id_e;
 1281:	       if((fl_load_menu_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 1282:	       {
 1283:	           hmi_menu_logic_load_dynamic_const_data(fl_load_menu_ptr);
 1284:	       }
 1285:	     #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 1286:	     hmi_menu_logic_load_absl_index(fl_load_menu_ptr);
 1287:	     #endif
 1288:	  }
 1289:	}
 1290:	/*============================================================================
 1291:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_load_dynamic_const_data
 1292:	** Visibility:       static
 1293:	** Description:      This function load the const dynamic data from config.inc to dynamic ram of menu
 1294:	** Invocation:       By the Menu core
 1295:	** Inputs:           MENU_CONTROL_T
 1296:	** Outputs:          
 1297:	** Critical Section: None.
 1298:	** Created:          05-Feb-2011 by csakthiv
 1299:	** Updated:          
 1300:	**==========================================================================*/
 1301:	static void hmi_menu_logic_load_dynamic_const_data(MENU_CONTROL_T const * p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1302:	{
 1303:	    UINT16 i,j;
 1304:	    MENU_DEF_T  const    * fl_menu_def_SP;
 1305:	    for(i = 0; i < TOTAL_DYNAMIC_MENU_ITEMS; i++)
 1306:	    {
 1307:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id == hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].menu_id)
 1308:	        {
 1309:	            fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 1310:	
 1311:	            for(j = 0; j < p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr; j++)
 1312:	            {
 1313:	                #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 1314:	                fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[j].item_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].entry_array[j].item_screen_id_U8;
 1315:	                fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[j].presentation_cb = hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].entry_array[j].presentation_cb;
 1316:	                #endif
 1317:	                fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[j].item_entry_type = hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].entry_array[j].item_entry_type;
 1318:	                fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[j].item_properties = hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].entry_array[j].item_properties;
 1319:	                fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[j].item_symbol = hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].entry_array[j].item_symbol;
 1320:	                #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEMS_WITH_TWO_ICONS
 1321:	                fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[j].item_symbol2 = hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].entry_array[j].item_symbol2;
 1322:	                #endif
 1323:	                fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[j].label = hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].entry_array[j].label;
 1324:	                fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[j].utype = hmi_menu_const_dynamic_menu_table[i].entry_array[j].utype;
 1325:	            }
 1326:	        }
 1327:	    }
 1328:	}               
 1329:	/*============================================================================
 1330:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance
 1331:	** Visibility:       global
 1332:	** Description:      This function is used to find the Menu Instance with
 1333:	**                   respect to Menu ID. It validtes the current available
 1334:	**                   Menu instance and returns the Instance.
 1335:	** Invocation:       By the Menu_interface.c
 1336:	** Inputs:           p_menu_id_U8 - Menu ID
 1337:	** Outputs:          Points to the Menu Instance.
 1338:	** Critical Section: None.
 1339:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1340:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1341:	**==========================================================================*/
 1342:	MENU_CONTROL_T  *hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8)
 1343:	{
 1344:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_no_U8;
 1345:	    MENU_CONTROL_T *fl_menu_inst_ID_ptr;
 1346:	
 1347:	    fl_menu_inst_ID_ptr = FNULL;
 1348:	    fl_menu_inst_no_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_menu_id_U8);
 1349:	    if(fl_menu_inst_no_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 1350:	    {
 1351:	        if(l_menu_instance_array[fl_menu_inst_no_U8].inst_status_BOOL != HMI_MENU_LOGIC_INST_INACTIVE)
 1352:	        {
 1353:	            if(l_menu_instance_array[fl_menu_inst_no_U8].menu_id == p_menu_id_U8)
 1354:	            {
 1355:	                fl_menu_inst_ID_ptr = &l_menu_instance_array[fl_menu_inst_no_U8];
 1356:	            }
 1357:	        }
 1358:	    }
 1359:	    return(fl_menu_inst_ID_ptr);
 1360:	}
 1361:	/*============================================================================
 1362:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_required_menu_instance
 1363:	** Visibility:       global
 1364:	** Description:      This function is used to get the required menu instance
 1365:	**                   from the available menu instance array
 1366:	** Invocation:       By the hmi application
 1367:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_inst_num_U8 - menu instance number
 1368:	** Outputs           MENU_CONTROL_T
 1369:	** Critical Section: None.
 1370:	** Created:          16-Dec-2010 by jpradeep
 1371:	** Updated:          16-Dec-2010 by jpradeep
 1372:	**==========================================================================*/
 1373:	MENU_CONTROL_T  *hmi_menu_logic_get_required_menu_instance(UINT8 p_menu_inst_num_U8)
 1374:	{
 1375:	  MENU_CONTROL_T *fl_ret_menu_inst_ptr;
 1376:	  fl_ret_menu_inst_ptr = FNULL;
 1377:	
 1378:	  if(p_menu_inst_num_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 1379:	  {
 1380:	    fl_ret_menu_inst_ptr = &l_menu_instance_array[p_menu_inst_num_U8];
 1381:	  }
 1382:	  return(fl_ret_menu_inst_ptr);
 1383:	}
 1384:	/*============================================================================
 1385:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_active_menu_instance_ptr
 1386:	** Visibility:       global
 1387:	** Description:      This function is used to get the active menu instance
 1388:	**                   from the available menu instance array
 1389:	** Invocation:       By the hmi application
 1390:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_inst_num_U8 - menu instance number
 1391:	** Outputs           MENU_CONTROL_T
 1392:	** Critical Section: None.
 1393:	** Created:          17-Oct-2016 by aperumal
 1394:	** Updated:          17-Oct-2016 by aperumal
 1395:	**==========================================================================*/
 1396:	MENU_CONTROL_T  *hmi_menu_logic_get_active_menu_instance_ptr(UINT8 p_menu_inst_num_U8)
 1397:	{
 1398:	  MENU_CONTROL_T *fl_ret_menu_inst_ptr;
 1399:	  fl_ret_menu_inst_ptr = FNULL;
 1400:	
 1401:	  if(p_menu_inst_num_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 1402:	  {
 1403:	      if(l_menu_instance_array[p_menu_inst_num_U8].inst_status_BOOL != HMI_MENU_LOGIC_INST_INACTIVE)
 1404:	      {
 1405:	        fl_ret_menu_inst_ptr = &l_menu_instance_array[p_menu_inst_num_U8];
 1406:	      }
 1407:	  }
 1408:	  return(fl_ret_menu_inst_ptr);
 1409:	}
 1410:	/*============================================================================
 1411:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_generic_focus_line_incr
 1412:	** Visibility:       global
 1413:	** Description:      This function process the Menu Down Action.
 1414:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 1415:	                        the Action.
 1416:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 1417:	                        corresponding states.
 1418:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 1419:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1420:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 1421:	** Critical Section: None.
 1422:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1423:	** Updated:
 1424:	**==========================================================================*/
 1425:	static void hmi_menu_logic_incr_focus_line(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1426:	{
 1427:	    if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 + HMI_MENU_LINE_INCREMENT)
 1428:	            >= p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines)
 1429:	    {
 1430:	        if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE) != 0)
 1431:	        {
 1432:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = 0;
 1433:	        }
 1434:	        else
 1435:	        {
 1436:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines
 1437:	                    - HMI_MENU_LINE_INCREMENT;
 1438:	        }
 1439:	    }
 1440:	    else if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_LTD_WND_FOCUS_ENTRY) != 0)
 1441:	    {
 1442:	       p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 + 1; 
 1443:	       if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 != (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr-1))
 1444:	       {
 1445:	          /* if not last entry in the menu, then decrement the focus line to the previous line */
 1446:	          /* if last entry, keep the focus line in the last entry itself */
 1447:	          if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 ==  (p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines -1))
 1448:	          {
 1449:	             /* after increment if focus line comes to last line, then decrement it by 1*/
 1450:	             p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 - 1; 
 1451:	          }   
 1452:	       }
 1453:	    }
 1454:	    else
 1455:	    {
 1456:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 + 1;
 1457:	    }
 1458:	}
 1459:	/*============================================================================
 1460:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_decr_focus_line
 1461:	** Visibility:       global
 1462:	** Description:      This function process the Menu Down Action.
 1463:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 1464:	                        the Action.
 1465:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 1466:	                        corresponding states.
 1467:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 1468:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1469:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 1470:	** Critical Section: None.
 1471:	** Created:
 1472:	** Updated:
 1473:	**==========================================================================*/
 1474:	
 1475:	static void hmi_menu_logic_decr_focus_line(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1476:	{
 1477:	    if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 == 0)
 1478:	    {
 1479:	        if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE) != 0)
 1480:	        {
 1481:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = (MIN_VALUE(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr,p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines) - 1);
 1482:	        }
 1483:	        else
 1484:	        {
 1485:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = 0;
 1486:	        }
 1487:	    }
 1488:	    else if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_LTD_WND_FOCUS_ENTRY) != 0)
 1489:	    {
 1490:	       p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 - 1; 
 1491:	       if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 != 0)
 1492:	       {
 1493:	          /* if not first entry in the menu, then increment the focus line to the next line */
 1494:	          /* if first entry, keep the focus line in the first entry itself */
 1495:	          if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 ==  0)
 1496:	          {
 1497:	             /* after decrement if focus line comes to first line, then increment it by 1*/
 1498:	             p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = 1; 
 1499:	          }   
 1500:	       }
 1501:	    }
 1502:	    else
 1503:	    {
 1504:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 - 1;
 1505:	    }
 1506:	}
 1507:	
 1508:	/*============================================================================
 1509:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_DOWN_action
 1510:	** Visibility:       global
 1511:	** Description:      This function process the Menu Down Action.
 1512:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 1513:	                        the Action.
 1514:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 1515:	                        corresponding states.
 1516:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 1517:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1518:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 1519:	** Critical Section: None.
 1520:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1521:	** Updated:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 1522:	**==========================================================================*/
 1523:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_DOWN_action(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1524:	{
 1525:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1526:	    UINT16                    fl_start_focus_entry_U16;
 1527:	    UINT16                    fl_start_focus_line_U16;
 1528:	#endif
 1529:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T          fl_cb_result_e;
 1530:	    MENU_ANIM_DATA_T          fl_menu_anim_data;
 1531:	    BOOLEAN                   fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL;
 1532:	
 1533:	    fl_cb_result_e  = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 1534:	
 1535:	    if(p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)
 1536:	    {
 1537:	    #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1538:	        fl_start_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 1539:	        fl_start_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 1540:	    #endif
 1541:	
 1542:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 1543:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 1544:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 1545:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1546:	
 1547:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr > 0)
 1548:	        {
 1549:	            if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_CLOCK_FORMAT) == 0)
 1550:	            {
 1551:	                if(((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 + 1)  >= p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr )&&
 1552:	                    ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) == 0)&&
 1553:	                    (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_NORMAL))
 1554:	                {
 1555:	
 1556:	                    /* do nothing*/
 1557:	                }
 1558:	                else
 1559:	                {
 1560:	                    if(hmi_menu_logic_dynamic_menu_cb_condition(MENU_CB_ACTION_DOWN,p_menu_cntrl_SP) != FALSE)
 1561:	                    {
 1562:	                        /* end of list is calculated before incr/decr focus entry. otherwise last line will  not displayed */
 1563:	                        /* Menu_completion will be posted before last line. MCA Issue*/ 
 1564:	                        fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL = hmi_menu_logic_is_it_end_of_list(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1565:	                        hmi_menu_logic_down_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1566:	                        fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_down_application_cb(p_menu_cntrl_SP,fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL);
 1567:	                    }
 1568:	                    else
 1569:	                    {
 1570:	                        hmi_menu_logic_down_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1571:	                    }
 1572:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_HISTORY_HANDLING_FEATURE
 1573:	                    hmi_menu_logic_update_history_info(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1574:	#endif
 1575:	                }
 1576:	            }
 1577:	            else
 1578:	            {
 1579:	                hmi_menu_logic_clock_application_cb(MENU_CB_ACTION_DOWN,p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1580:	            }
 1581:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1582:	            if((fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)&& (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state != MENU_STATE_FAST))
 1583:	            {
 1584:	                /* For wait and fast scrool animation is run from menu core chart. if below funct. is called
 1585:	                then animation will be changed to focus move up/down*/
 1586:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_get_down_animation(fl_start_focus_entry_U16,fl_start_focus_line_U16,p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1587:	            }
 1588:	#endif
 1589:	        }
 1590:	
 1591:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 1592:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 1593:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 1594:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1595:	
 1596:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1597:	    #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 1598:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines == NO_DISP_LINES_1)
 1599:	        {
 1600:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 1601:	            (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 1602:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 1603:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 1604:	
 1605:	        }
 1606:	        else
 1607:	    #endif
 1608:	            {
 1609:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 1610:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 1611:	        }
 1612:	#else
 1613:	        if(MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT == fl_cb_result_e)
 1614:	        {
 1615:	            hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 1616:	        }
 1617:	        else
 1618:	        {
 1619:	            hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 1620:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1621:	                , ANIM_MAX_ID
 1622:	#endif
 1623:	                , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 1624:	        }
 1625:	#endif
 1626:	    }
 1627:	    return (fl_cb_result_e);
 1628:	}
 1629:	/*============================================================================
 1630:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_UP_action
 1631:	** Visibility:       global
 1632:	** Description:      This function process the Menu Up Action.
 1633:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 1634:	                        the Action.
 1635:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 1636:	                        corresponding states.
 1637:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 1638:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1639:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 1640:	** Critical Section: None.
 1641:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 1642:	** Updated:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 1643:	**==========================================================================*/
 1644:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_UP_action(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1645:	{
 1646:	    #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1647:	    UINT16                    fl_start_focus_entry_U16;
 1648:	    UINT16                    fl_start_focus_line_U16;
 1649:	    #endif
 1650:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T          fl_cb_result_e;
 1651:	    MENU_ANIM_DATA_T          fl_menu_anim_data;
 1652:	    BOOLEAN                   fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL;
 1653:	
 1654:	    fl_cb_result_e  = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 1655:	
 1656:	    if(p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)
 1657:	    {
 1658:	        #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1659:	            fl_start_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 1660:	            fl_start_focus_line_U16  = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 1661:	        #endif
 1662:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 1663:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 1664:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 1665:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1666:	
 1667:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr > 0)
 1668:	        {
 1669:	            if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_CLOCK_FORMAT) == 0)
 1670:	            {
 1671:	                if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 == 0 )&&
 1672:	                    ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) == 0)&&
 1673:	                    (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_NORMAL))
 1674:	                {
 1675:	                    /* do nothing*/
 1676:	                }
 1677:	                else
 1678:	                {
 1679:	                    if(hmi_menu_logic_dynamic_menu_cb_condition(MENU_CB_ACTION_UP,p_menu_cntrl_SP) != FALSE)
 1680:	                    {
 1681:	                        /* this was done like this because applicaiton handles preset and cd track list menu differently
 1682:	                        * For cd tracklist menu,application handles incrementing focus entry,but for preset list menu
 1683:	                        * application does not increment focus entry after window move
 1684:	                        */
 1685:	                        /* start of list is calculated before incr/decr focus entry. otherwise last line will  not displayed */
 1686:	                        /* Menu_completion will be posted before last line. MCA Issue*/ 
 1687:	                        fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL = hmi_menu_logic_is_it_start_of_list(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1688:	                        hmi_menu_logic_up_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1689:	                        fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_up_application_cb(p_menu_cntrl_SP,fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL);
 1690:	                    }
 1691:	                    else
 1692:	                    {
 1693:	                        hmi_menu_logic_up_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1694:	                    }
 1695:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_HISTORY_HANDLING_FEATURE
 1696:	                    hmi_menu_logic_update_history_info(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1697:	#endif
 1698:	                }
 1699:	            }
 1700:	            else
 1701:	            {
 1702:	                hmi_menu_logic_clock_application_cb(MENU_CB_ACTION_UP,p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1703:	            }
 1704:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1705:	            if((fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)&& (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state != MENU_STATE_FAST))
 1706:	            {
 1707:	                /* For wait and fast scrool animation is run from menu core chart. if below funct. is called
 1708:	                then animation will be changed to focus move up/down*/
 1709:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_get_up_animation(fl_start_focus_entry_U16,fl_start_focus_line_U16,p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1710:	            }
 1711:	#endif
 1712:	        }
 1713:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 1714:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 1715:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 1716:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 1717:	
 1718:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1719:	    #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 1720:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines == NO_DISP_LINES_1)
 1721:	        {
 1722:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 1723:	            (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 1724:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 1725:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 1726:	        }
 1727:	        else
 1728:	    #endif
 1729:	            {
 1730:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 1731:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 1732:	        }
 1733:	#else
 1734:	        if(MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT == fl_cb_result_e)
 1735:	        {
 1736:	            hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 1737:	        }
 1738:	        else
 1739:	        {
 1740:	            hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 1741:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1742:	                , ANIM_MAX_ID
 1743:	#endif
 1744:	                , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 1745:	        }
 1746:	#endif
 1747:	    }
 1748:	    return (fl_cb_result_e);
 1749:	}
 1750:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 1751:	/*============================================================================
 1752:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_down_animation
 1753:	** Visibility:       menu core
 1754:	** Description:      This function decides the type of animation to be triggered for down button press
 1755:	**                   based on focus entry and focus line.
 1756:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 1757:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1758:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 1759:	** Critical Section: None.
 1760:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 1761:	** Updated:
 1762:	**==========================================================================*/
 1763:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_get_down_animation(UINT16 fl_start_focus_entry_U16,UINT16 fl_start_focus_line_U16, MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1764:	{
 1765:	    UINT16 fl_start_page_no_U16;
 1766:	    UINT16 fl_new_page_no_U16;
 1767:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 1768:	    if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE) != 0)
 1769:	    {
 1770:	        fl_start_page_no_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(fl_start_focus_entry_U16, p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 1771:	        fl_new_page_no_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16, p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 1772:	
 1773:	        if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 > fl_start_focus_entry_U16 )&&
 1774:	            (fl_start_page_no_U16 == fl_new_page_no_U16))
 1775:	        {
 1776:	
 1777:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_FOCUS_MOVE_DOWN_ANM;
 1778:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 1779:	                MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 1780:	            #endif
 1781:	        }
 1782:	        if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 > fl_start_focus_entry_U16 )&&
 1783:	            (fl_start_page_no_U16 != fl_new_page_no_U16))
 1784:	        {
 1785:	            /* For static menu window move down*/
 1786:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WNDW_MOVE_DOWN_ANM;
 1787:	        }
 1788:	    }
 1789:	    else
 1790:	    {
 1791:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 > fl_start_focus_entry_U16 )
 1792:	        {
 1793:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_FOCUS_MOVE_DOWN_ANM;
 1794:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 1795:	                MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 1796:	            #endif
 1797:	        }
 1798:	        /* below lines wont work if HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT is enabled */
 1799:	        /* current there is no requirment for HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT in non MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE menu */
 1800:	        /* e.g, MFD TVM MCA */
 1801:	        if(((fl_start_focus_line_U16  > p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16)||((fl_start_focus_line_U16 + 1)  >= p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines))&&
 1802:	            (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 > fl_start_focus_entry_U16 ))
 1803:	        {
 1804:	            /* For static menu window move down*/
 1805:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WNDW_MOVE_DOWN_ANM;
 1806:	        }
 1807:	    }
 1808:	    /* page comparison is not required as wrap around can happen within single page also fe = 4 disp.line =4.*/
 1809:	    if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < fl_start_focus_entry_U16)
 1810:	    {
 1811:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr != 1)
 1812:	        {
 1813:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WRAP_RND_DOWN_ANM;
 1814:	        }
 1815:	        else
 1816:	        {
 1817:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 1818:	        }
 1819:	    }
 1820:	    return fl_cb_result_e;
 1821:	}
 1822:	
 1823:	/*============================================================================
 1824:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_up_animation
 1825:	** Visibility:       menu core
 1826:	** Description:      This function decides the type of animation to be triggered for up button press
 1827:	**                   based on focus entry and focus line.
 1828:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 1829:	** Inputs/:          fl_start_focus_entry_U16, fl_start_focus_line_U16,p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1830:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 1831:	** Critical Section: None.
 1832:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 1833:	** Updated:
 1834:	**==========================================================================*/
 1835:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_get_up_animation(UINT16 fl_start_focus_entry_U16,UINT16 fl_start_focus_line_U16, MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1836:	{
 1837:	
 1838:	    UINT16 fl_start_page_no_U16;
 1839:	    UINT16 fl_new_page_no_U16;
 1840:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 1841:	    if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE) != 0)
 1842:	    {
 1843:	        fl_start_page_no_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(fl_start_focus_entry_U16, p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 1844:	        fl_new_page_no_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16, p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 1845:	
 1846:	        if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < fl_start_focus_entry_U16 )&&
 1847:	            (fl_start_page_no_U16 == fl_new_page_no_U16))
 1848:	        {
 1849:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_FOCUS_MOVE_UP_ANM;
 1850:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 1851:	                MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 1852:	            #endif
 1853:	        }
 1854:	
 1855:	        if((fl_start_page_no_U16 != fl_new_page_no_U16)&&
 1856:	            (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < fl_start_focus_entry_U16))
 1857:	        {
 1858:	            /* For static menu window move down*/
 1859:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WNDW_MOVE_UP_ANM;
 1860:	        }
 1861:	    }
 1862:	    else
 1863:	    {
 1864:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < fl_start_focus_entry_U16 )
 1865:	        {
 1866:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_FOCUS_MOVE_UP_ANM;
 1867:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 1868:	                MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 1869:	            #endif
 1870:	        }
 1871:	        /* below lines wont work if HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT is enabled */
 1872:	        /* current there is no requirment for HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT in non MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE menu */
 1873:	        /* e.g, MFD TVM MCA */
 1874:	        if((fl_start_focus_line_U16 == 0)&&
 1875:	            (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < fl_start_focus_entry_U16 ))
 1876:	        {
 1877:	            /* For static menu window move down*/
 1878:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WNDW_MOVE_UP_ANM;
 1879:	        }
 1880:	    }
 1881:	    /* page comparison is not required as wrap around can happen within single page also fe = 4 disp.line =4.*/
 1882:	    if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 > fl_start_focus_entry_U16)
 1883:	    {
 1884:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr != 1)
 1885:	        {
 1886:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WRAP_RND_UP_ANM;
 1887:	        }
 1888:	        else
 1889:	        {
 1890:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 1891:	        }
 1892:	    }
 1893:	    return fl_cb_result_e;
 1894:	}
 1895:	#endif
 1896:	
 1897:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_HISTORY_HANDLING_FEATURE
 1898:	/*============================================================================
 1899:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_updated_history_info
 1900:	** Visibility:       global
 1901:	** Description:      This function calls application interface to update history info(menu id and focus entry)
 1902:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_core_logic
 1903:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1904:	** Outputs
 1905:	** Critical Section: None.
 1906:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 1907:	** Updated:
 1908:	**==========================================================================*/
 1909:	static void hmi_menu_logic_update_history_info(MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1910:	{
 1911:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_HISTORY_MENU) != 0)
 1912:	    {
 1913:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 1914:	        HMI_MENU_HISTORY_STORE_MENU_ITEM(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP));
 1915:	#else
 1916:	        HMI_MENU_HISTORY_STORE_MENU_ITEM(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16);
 1917:	#endif
 1918:	    }
 1919:	}
 1920:	#endif
 1921:	/*============================================================================
 1922:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_down_focus_entry
 1923:	** Visibility:       global
 1924:	** Description:      This function do changes in focus entry for down button press
 1925:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 1926:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1927:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 1928:	** Critical Section: None.
 1929:	** Created:          24-Nov-csakthiv
 1930:	** Updated:
 1931:	**==========================================================================*/
 1932:	
 1933:	static void hmi_menu_logic_down_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1934:	{
 1935:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 1936:	    UINT16 fl_end_focus_entry_U16;
 1937:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) != 0)
 1938:	    {
 1939:	        /*search for the complete menu item*/
 1940:	        fl_end_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 1941:	    }
 1942:	    else
 1943:	    {
 1944:	        fl_end_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr;
 1945:	    }
 1946:	#endif
 1947:	    hmi_menu_logic_incr_till_valid_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1948:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 1949:	        , fl_end_focus_entry_U16
 1950:	#endif
 1951:	        );
 1952:	}
 1953:	/*============================================================================
 1954:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_up_focus_entry
 1955:	** Visibility:       global
 1956:	** Description:      This function do changes in focus entry for up button press
 1957:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 1958:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1959:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 1960:	** Critical Section: None.
 1961:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 1962:	** Updated:
 1963:	**==========================================================================*/
 1964:	static void hmi_menu_logic_up_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 1965:	{
 1966:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 1967:	    UINT16 fl_end_focus_entry_U16;
 1968:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) != 0)
 1969:	    {
 1970:	        /*search for the complete menu item*/
 1971:	        fl_end_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 1972:	    }
 1973:	    else
 1974:	    {
 1975:	        fl_end_focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 1976:	    }
 1977:	#endif
 1978:	    hmi_menu_logic_decr_till_valid_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1979:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 1980:	        ,fl_end_focus_entry_U16
 1981:	#endif
 1982:	        );
 1983:	}
 1984:	
 1985:	/*============================================================================
 1986:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_incr_till_valid_focus_entry
 1987:	** Visibility:       global
 1988:	** Description:      This function decrement the focus entry till valid focus entry or fl_end_focus_entry_U16 is reached.
 1989:	**                   Wrap around property is considered for decrement.
 1990:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_core_logic
 1991:	** Inputs:           p_menu_cntrl_SP,fl_end_focus_entry_U16
 1992:	** Outputs           void
 1993:	** Critical Section: None.
 1994:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 1995:	** Updated:
 1996:	**==========================================================================*/
 1997:	static void hmi_menu_logic_incr_till_valid_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP
 1998:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 1999:	    , UINT16 fl_end_focus_entry_U16
 2000:	#endif
 2001:	    )
 2002:	{
 2003:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 2004:	    BOOLEAN fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL;
 2005:	    BOOLEAN fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL;
 2006:	    UINT16 fl_start_focus_entry_U16;
 2007:	    UINT16 fl_start_focus_line_U16;
 2008:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)
 2009:	    UINT16  fl_page_start_focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 2010:	    BOOLEAN fl_page_number_changed_BOOL = FALSE;
 2011:	    UINT8 fl_no_of_pages_changed_U8 = 0;
 2012:	    UINT16 fl_prev_page_number_U16;
 2013:	    UINT16 fl_start_page_number_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16, p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 2014:	    UINT16 fl_new_page_number_U16;
 2015:	    fl_prev_page_number_U16 = fl_start_page_number_U16;
 2016:	#endif
 2017:	    fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL = FALSE;
 2018:	    fl_start_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2019:	    fl_start_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2020:	    fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL = hmi_menu_logic_incr_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2021:	    while ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 != fl_end_focus_entry_U16) && (fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL == FALSE))
 2022:	    {
 2023:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)
 2024:	        if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE) != 0)
 2025:	        {
 2026:	            fl_new_page_number_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16,p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 2027:	            if(fl_prev_page_number_U16 != fl_new_page_number_U16)
 2028:	            {
 2029:	                /*focus entry is valid even when element inside it are greyed out as we need to show the item to user*/
 2030:	                /*otherwise greyed out item will never be visible */
 2031:	                if(fl_page_number_changed_BOOL != TRUE)
 2032:	                {
 2033:	                    fl_page_start_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16; 
 2034:	                }
 2035:	                fl_page_number_changed_BOOL = TRUE;
 2036:	                fl_no_of_pages_changed_U8++;
 2037:	                /* if more than one page is changed then replace focus entry with start focus entry */
 2038:	                if(fl_no_of_pages_changed_U8 > 1)
 2039:	                {
 2040:	                    p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = fl_page_start_focus_entry_U16;
 2041:	                    hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2042:	                    fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL =  TRUE;
 2043:	                    break;
 2044:	                }
 2045:	            }
 2046:	        }
 2047:	#endif
 2048:	        if (hmi_menu_logic_is_focus_entry_valid(p_menu_cntrl_SP) == FALSE)
 2049:	        {
 2050:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)
 2051:	            /* here also check for MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE is required. But not done to avoid complex in reading*/
 2052:	            /* there wont be any impact even it was not added */
 2053:	            fl_prev_page_number_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16,p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 2054:	#endif
 2055:	            fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL = hmi_menu_logic_incr_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2056:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)
 2057:	            if (((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE) != 0)&&
 2058:	                ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) != 0))
 2059:	            {
 2060:	                /* If wrap around is enabled, then dont exit for loop until the same focus entry is reached */
 2061:	                /* if page based focus greyout is required then that menu should defintely be wrap around menu */
 2062:	                fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL = FALSE;
 2063:	            }
 2064:	#endif
 2065:	        }
 2066:	        else
 2067:	        {
 2068:	            /* if focus entry is valid then exist the for loop*/
 2069:	            fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL =  TRUE;
 2070:	            break;
 2071:	        }
 2072:	    }
 2073:	    if (((fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL == TRUE) && ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) == 0))
 2074:	        || (fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL == FALSE))
 2075:	    {
 2076:	        /* since no wrap around revert back to last valid focus entry */
 2077:	        /* it entered here since the focus entry is not valid */
 2078:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = fl_start_focus_entry_U16;
 2079:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = fl_start_focus_line_U16;
 2080:	    }
 2081:	#else
 2082:	    (void)hmi_menu_logic_incr_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2083:	#endif
 2084:	
 2085:	}
 2086:	/*============================================================================
 2087:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_decr_till_valid_focus_entry
 2088:	** Visibility:       global
 2089:	** Description:      This function decrement the focus entry till valid focus entry or fl_end_focus_entry_U16 is reached.
 2090:	**                   Wrap around property is considered for decrement.
 2091:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 2092:	** Inputs:           p_menu_cntrl_SP,fl_end_focus_entry_U16
 2093:	** Outputs           void
 2094:	** Critical Section: None.
 2095:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2096:	** Updated:
 2097:	**==========================================================================*/
 2098:	    static void hmi_menu_logic_decr_till_valid_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2099:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 2100:	        ,UINT16 fl_end_focus_entry_U16
 2101:	#endif
 2102:	        )
 2103:	{
 2104:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 2105:	    BOOLEAN fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL = FALSE;
 2106:	    UINT16 fl_start_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2107:	    UINT16 fl_start_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2108:	    BOOLEAN fl_menu_start_of_list_BOOL;
 2109:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)
 2110:	    UINT16 fl_new_page_number_U16;
 2111:	    BOOLEAN fl_page_number_changed_BOOL = FALSE;
 2112:	    UINT8   fl_no_of_pages_changed_U8 = 0;
 2113:	    UINT16  fl_page_start_focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 2114:	    UINT16 fl_start_page_number_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16, p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 2115:	#endif
 2116:	    fl_menu_start_of_list_BOOL = hmi_menu_logic_decr_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2117:	    while((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 != fl_end_focus_entry_U16)&&(fl_menu_start_of_list_BOOL == FALSE))
 2118:	    {
 2119:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)
 2120:	        if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE) != 0)
 2121:	        {
 2122:	            fl_new_page_number_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16,p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 2123:	            if(fl_start_page_number_U16 != fl_new_page_number_U16)
 2124:	            {
 2125:	                /*focus entry is valid even when element inside it are greyed out as we need to show the item to user*/
 2126:	                /*otherwise greyed out item will never be visible */
 2127:	                if(fl_page_number_changed_BOOL != TRUE)
 2128:	                {
 2129:	                    fl_page_start_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2130:	                }
 2131:	                fl_page_number_changed_BOOL = TRUE;
 2132:	                fl_no_of_pages_changed_U8++;
 2133:	                /* if more than one page is changed then replace focus entry with start focus entry */
 2134:	                if(fl_no_of_pages_changed_U8 > 1)
 2135:	                {
 2136:	                    p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = fl_page_start_focus_entry_U16;
 2137:	                    hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2138:	                    fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL =  TRUE;
 2139:	                    break;
 2140:	                }
 2141:	            }
 2142:	        }
 2143:	#endif
 2144:	
 2145:	        if (hmi_menu_logic_is_focus_entry_valid(p_menu_cntrl_SP) == FALSE)
 2146:	        {
 2147:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)
 2148:	            /* here also check for MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE is required. But not done to avoid complex in reading*/
 2149:	            /* there wont be any impact even it was not added */
 2150:	            fl_start_page_number_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16,p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 2151:	#endif
 2152:	            fl_menu_start_of_list_BOOL = hmi_menu_logic_decr_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2153:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)
 2154:	            if (((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FAST_WINDOW_CHANGE) != 0)&&
 2155:	                ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) != 0))
 2156:	            {
 2157:	                /* If wrap around is enabled, then dont exit for loop until the same focus entry is reached */
 2158:	                /* if page based focus greyout is required then that menu should defintely be wrap around menu */
 2159:	                fl_menu_start_of_list_BOOL = FALSE;
 2160:	            }
 2161:	#endif
 2162:	        }
 2163:	        else
 2164:	        {
 2165:	            /* valid entry found*/
 2166:	            fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL =  TRUE;
 2167:	            break;
 2168:	        }
 2169:	    }
 2170:	    /* since it is difficult to compare signed and unsigned int in c , below code is added. otherwise in Up_focus_entry()*/
 2171:	    /* fl_end_focus_entry_U16 should be set to -1 instead of 0*/
 2172:	    if((fl_menu_found_valid_focus_entry_BOOL == FALSE)&&(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 == fl_end_focus_entry_U16))
 2173:	    {
 2174:	        if (hmi_menu_logic_is_focus_entry_valid(p_menu_cntrl_SP) == FALSE)
 2175:	        {
 2176:	            /* if no valid focus entry is found untill the end of focus entry, then keep the focus entry and focus line as start*/
 2177:	            /* since no wrap around revert back to last valid focus entry */
 2178:	            /* it entered here since the focus entry is not valid */
 2179:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = fl_start_focus_entry_U16;
 2180:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = fl_start_focus_line_U16;
 2181:	        }
 2182:	        else
 2183:	        {
 2184:	            /* do nothing keep the focus entry same since it is valid. FE = 0 without wrap around */
 2185:	        }
 2186:	    }
 2187:	#else
 2188:	    (void)hmi_menu_logic_decr_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2189:	#endif
 2190:	}
 2191:	
 2192:	/*============================================================================
 2193:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_incr_focus_entry
 2194:	** Visibility:       global
 2195:	** Description:      This function increment the focus entry and return true if end of list is reached.
 2196:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 2197:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2198:	** Outputs           TRUE end of list,false not end of list
 2199:	** Critical Section: None.
 2200:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2201:	** Updated:
 2202:	**==========================================================================*/
 2203:	static BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_incr_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 2204:	{
 2205:	    BOOLEAN fl_end_of_list_BOOL;
 2206:	
 2207:	    UINT16 fl_start_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2208:	
 2209:	    fl_end_of_list_BOOL = FALSE;
 2210:	
 2211:	    if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 + 1) >= p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr)
 2212:	    {
 2213:	        if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) != 0)
 2214:	        {
 2215:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 2216:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = 0;
 2217:	        }
 2218:	        else
 2219:	        {
 2220:	            /* no wrap around . also reached the end of the list. so do nothing */
 2221:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = fl_start_focus_entry_U16;
 2222:	            fl_end_of_list_BOOL = TRUE;
 2223:	        }
 2224:	    }
 2225:	    else
 2226:	    {
 2227:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 + 1;
 2228:	        hmi_menu_logic_incr_focus_line(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2229:	    }
 2230:	    return fl_end_of_list_BOOL;
 2231:	}
 2232:	/*============================================================================
 2233:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_decr_focus_entry
 2234:	** Visibility:       global
 2235:	** Description:      This function decrement the focus entry and return true if start of list is reached.
 2236:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_core_logic
 2237:	** Inputs:           p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2238:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 2239:	** Critical Section: None.
 2240:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2241:	** Updated:
 2242:	**==========================================================================*/
 2243:	static BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_decr_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 2244:	{
 2245:	    BOOLEAN fl_start_of_list_BOOL;
 2246:	
 2247:	    UINT16 fl_start_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2248:	
 2249:	    fl_start_of_list_BOOL = FALSE;
 2250:	
 2251:	    if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 == 0)
 2252:	    {
 2253:	        if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) != 0)
 2254:	        {
 2255:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr - 1);
 2256:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CENTRE_FOCUS_LINE
 2257:	            /* Used in L538,TVM and P552 */
 2258:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = (MIN_VALUE(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr,p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines) - 1);
 2259:	            #else
 2260:	            /* Used in P42 */
 2261:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 % p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 2262:	            #endif
 2263:	        }
 2264:	        else
 2265:	        {
 2266:	            /* no wrap around . also reached the start of the list. so do nothing */
 2267:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = fl_start_focus_entry_U16;
 2268:	            fl_start_of_list_BOOL = TRUE;
 2269:	        }
 2270:	    }
 2271:	    else
 2272:	    {
 2273:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 - 1;
 2274:	        hmi_menu_logic_decr_focus_line(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2275:	    }
 2276:	    return fl_start_of_list_BOOL;
 2277:	}
 2278:	/*============================================================================
 2279:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_is_focus_entry_valid
 2280:	** Visibility:       global
 2281:	** Description:      This function process the Menu Down Action.
 2282:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 2283:	                        the Action.
 2284:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 2285:	                        corresponding states.
 2286:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 2287:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2288:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 2289:	** Critical Section: None.
 2290:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2291:	** Updated:
 2292:	**==========================================================================*/
 2293:	static BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_is_focus_entry_valid(MENU_CONTROL_T const * p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 2294:	{
 2295:	    /* skipping the greyed out item is not applicable for dynamic menu as the size of dynamic menu is not equal to entry array ram(normally 10) */
 2296:	    /* but in c346 mca it is required for media device list menu. but they are configuring both the ram entry array size(16) and number of entry(16) as same*/
 2297:	    BOOLEAN fl_focus_entry_valid_BOOL = TRUE;
 2298:	    #if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 2299:	    UINT16 fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16;
 2300:	    MENU_DEF_T  const         * fl_menu_def_SP;
 2301:	    fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 2302:	    #endif
 2303:	    if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr)
 2304:	    {
 2305:	#if defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT) && defined(HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE)
 2306:	    /* if it is dynamic CD menu where RAM of entry array is less than actual number of entry,then item cb should be configured by*/
 2307:	    /* application.*/
 2308:	        if(fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL)
 2309:	        {
 2310:	            fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2311:	        if(TESTBIT(fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16,BIT7))
 2312:	        {
 2313:	           /* if greyed out then entry is invalid ie. skip the entry*/
