 2314:	           fl_focus_entry_valid_BOOL = FALSE;
 2315:	        }
 2316:	        }
 2317:	#endif
 2318:	    }
 2319:	    return fl_focus_entry_valid_BOOL;
 2320:	}
 2321:	/*============================================================================
 2322:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_clock_application_cb
 2323:	** Visibility:       global
 2324:	** Description:      This function is to call the application callback for clock property.
 2325:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 2326:	** Inputs/:          fl_cb_result_e, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2327:	** Outputs           void
 2328:	** Critical Section: None.
 2329:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2330:	** Updated:          15-Feb-2014 by csakthiv
 2331:	**==========================================================================*/
 2332:	static void hmi_menu_logic_clock_application_cb(MENU_CB_RESULT_T fl_cb_result_e,
 2333:	        MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 2334:	{
 2335:	    MENU_DEF_T const * fl_menu_def_SP;
 2336:	
 2337:	    if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL) && (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr > 0))
 2338:	    {
 2339:	        fl_menu_def_SP =&hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 2340:	        if(fl_menu_def_SP->cb != FNULL)
 2341:	        {
 2342:	            if (fl_cb_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_DOWN)
 2343:	            {
 2344:	                if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_NORMAL)
 2345:	                {
 2346:	                        (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_INCREMENT,
 2347:	                        p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2348:	                }
 2349:	                else
 2350:	                {
 2351:	                        (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_INCREMENT_FAST,
 2352:	                        p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2353:	                }
 2354:	            }
 2355:	            else if (fl_cb_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_UP)
 2356:	            {
 2357:	                if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_NORMAL)
 2358:	                {
 2359:	                        (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_DECREMENT,
 2360:	                        p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2361:	                }
 2362:	                else
 2363:	                {
 2364:	                        (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_DECREMENT_FAST,
 2365:	                        p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2366:	                }
 2367:	            }
 2368:	            else
 2369:	            {
 2370:	                /* do nothing */
 2371:	            }
 2372:	        }
 2373:	    }
 2374:	}
 2375:	/*============================================================================
 2376:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_dynamic_menu_cb_condition
 2377:	** Visibility:       global
 2378:	** Description:      This funciton calls the application callback for dynamic menu .
 2379:	** Invocation:       hmi_menu_core
 2380:	** Inputs/:          fl_cb_result_e, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2381:	** Outputs           TRUE/FALSE
 2382:	** Critical Section: None.
 2383:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2384:	** Updated:
 2385:	**==========================================================================*/
 2386:	static BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_dynamic_menu_cb_condition(MENU_CB_RESULT_T fl_cb_result_e, MENU_CONTROL_T const * p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 2387:	{
 2388:	    BOOLEAN fl_menu_dynamic_cb_BOOL = FALSE;
 2389:	    if(MENU_CB_ACTION_DOWN == fl_cb_result_e)
 2390:	    {
 2391:	        if (((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 + 1) >= MIN_VALUE(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr,p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines))
 2392:	                || ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 + 1)>= p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr)
 2393:	                || (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_FAST))
 2394:	        {
 2395:	            if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 2396:	            {
 2397:	                /* if all the above conditions are satisfied, then dynamic menu callback has to be called*/
 2398:	                /* in that case menu core ideally should not increment/decrement focus entry/line*/
 2399:	                /* for CD dyanmic menu application increment/decrement focus entry, but for preset list menu*/
 2400:	                /* application does not increment/decrement focus entry. so called the incr/decr always and*/
 2401:	                /* then application callback is called and app will incre/decr the focus entry if required*/
 2402:	                fl_menu_dynamic_cb_BOOL =  TRUE;
 2403:	            }
 2404:	        }
 2405:	    }
 2406:	    else if (MENU_CB_ACTION_UP == fl_cb_result_e)
 2407:	    {
 2408:	        if (((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 == 0))
 2409:	            || (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_FAST))
 2410:	        {
 2411:	            if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 2412:	            {
 2413:	                /* if all the above conditions are satisfied, then dynamic menu callback has to be called*/
 2414:	                /* in that case menu core ideally should not increment/decrement focus entry/line*/
 2415:	                /* for CD dyanmic menu application increment/decrement focus entry, but for preset list menu*/
 2416:	                /* application does not increment/decrement focus entry. so called the incr/decr always and*/
 2417:	                /* then application callback is called and app will incre/decr the focus entry if required*/
 2418:	                fl_menu_dynamic_cb_BOOL =  TRUE;
 2419:	            }
 2420:	        }
 2421:	    }
 2422:	    else
 2423:	    {
 2424:	    }
 2425:	    return fl_menu_dynamic_cb_BOOL;
 2426:	}
 2427:	/*============================================================================
 2428:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_down_application_cb
 2429:	** Visibility:       global
 2430:	** Description:      This function calls the callback of application with menu actions based on focus entry/line
 2431:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_core_logic
 2432:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2433:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 2434:	** Critical Section: None.
 2435:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2436:	** Updated:
 2437:	**==========================================================================*/
 2438:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_down_application_cb(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP, BOOLEAN fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL)
 2439:	{
 2440:	    MENU_DEF_T const * fl_menu_def_SP;
 2441:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 2442:	
 2443:	    fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 2444:	    /* callback only for dynamic menu in both normal and fast scroll mode*/
 2445:	    if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 2446:	    {
 2447:	        if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 2448:	        {
 2449:	                if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_NORMAL)
 2450:	                {
 2451:	                    fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_DOWN, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2452:	                    l_end_of_fast_scroll_BOOL = FALSE;
 2453:	                }
 2454:	                else
 2455:	                {
 2456:	                    if (fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL != FALSE)
 2457:	                    {
 2458:	                        /* End of the list */
 2459:	                        fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_COMPLETION, p_menu_cntrl_SP,MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2460:	                        /*End of animation reached, Action completed provided to application
 2461:	                             to build full display and set the flag as true*/
 2462:	                        l_end_of_fast_scroll_BOOL = TRUE;
 2463:	                    }
 2464:	                    else
 2465:	                    {
 2466:	                        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines - 1;
 2467:	                        fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_DOWN_FAST, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2468:	                    }
 2469:	                }
 2470:	            }
 2471:	        }
 2472:	    return fl_cb_result_e;
 2473:	}
 2474:	/*============================================================================
 2475:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_up_application_cb
 2476:	** Visibility:       menu core
 2477:	** Description:      This function calls the callback of application with menu actions based on focus entry/line
 2478:	** Invocation:       hmi_menu_core
 2479:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2480:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 2481:	** Critical Section: None.
 2482:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2483:	** Updated:
 2484:	**==========================================================================*/
 2485:	
 2486:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_up_application_cb(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP, BOOLEAN fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL)
 2487:	{
 2488:	    MENU_DEF_T const * fl_menu_def_SP;
 2489:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 2490:	
 2491:	    fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 2492:	    /* callback only for dynamic menu in both normal and fast scroll mode*/
 2493:	    if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 2494:	    {
 2495:	        if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 2496:	        {
 2497:	            
 2498:	                if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_NORMAL)
 2499:	                {
 2500:	                    fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_UP, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2501:	                    l_end_of_fast_scroll_BOOL = FALSE;
 2502:	                }
 2503:	                else
 2504:	                {
 2505:	                    if (fl_menu_fs_end_of_list_BOOL != FALSE)
 2506:	                    {
 2507:	                        /* End of the list */
 2508:	                        fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_COMPLETION, p_menu_cntrl_SP,MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2509:	                        /*End of animation reached, Action completed provided to application
 2510:	                             to build full display and set the flag as true*/
 2511:	                        l_end_of_fast_scroll_BOOL = TRUE;
 2512:	                    }
 2513:	                    else
 2514:	                    {
 2515:	                        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines - 1;
 2516:	                        fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_UP_FAST, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2517:	                }
 2518:	            }
 2519:	        }
 2520:	    }
 2521:	    return fl_cb_result_e;
 2522:	}
 2523:	/*============================================================================
 2524:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_is_it_end_of_list
 2525:	** Visibility:       Local
 2526:	** Description:      Interface to identity whether current focus entry/focus line is in end of list
 2527:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic_DOWN_action ()
 2528:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2529:	** Outputs           TRUE/FALSE
 2530:	** Critical Section: None.
 2531:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2532:	** Updated:          
 2533:	**==========================================================================*/
 2534:	static BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_is_it_end_of_list(MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 2535:	{
 2536:	    BOOLEAN fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL;
 2537:	    UINT16 fl_menu_focus_entryU16;
 2538:	
 2539:	    fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL = FALSE;
 2540:	
 2541:	    fl_menu_focus_entryU16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr -1-p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2542:	    if(fl_menu_focus_entryU16 == 0)
 2543:	    {
 2544:	        fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL = TRUE;
 2545:	    }
 2546:	
 2547:	    return fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL;
 2548:	
 2549:	}
 2550:	/*============================================================================
 2551:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_is_it_start_of_list
 2552:	** Visibility:       global
 2553:	** Description:      Interface to identity whether current focus entry/focus line is in start of list
 2554:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic_UP_action ()
 2555:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2556:	** Outputs           TRUE/FALSE
 2557:	** Critical Section: None.
 2558:	** Created:          24-Nov-2013 by csakthiv
 2559:	** Updated:
 2560:	**==========================================================================*/
 2561:	static BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_is_it_start_of_list(MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 2562:	{
 2563:	    BOOLEAN fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL;
 2564:	
 2565:	    fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL = FALSE;
 2566:	    /* below check is modified from previous version verify it again*/
 2567:	    /* previous check           fl_dec_focus_entry_U16 = MIN_VALUE(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16, \
 2568:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16+p_menu_cntrl_SP->line_fast_inc);*/
 2569:	
 2570:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 == 0)&&
 2571:	            (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 == 0))
 2572:	    {
 2573:	        fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL = TRUE;
 2574:	    }
 2575:	    return fl_menu_end_of_list_BOOL;
 2576:	}
 2577:	/*============================================================================
 2578:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_LEFT_action
 2579:	** Visibility:       global
 2580:	** Description:      This function process the Menu Left Action.
 2581:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 2582:	                        the Action.
 2583:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 2584:	                        corresponding states.
 2585:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 2586:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2587:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 2588:	** Critical Section: None.
 2589:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 2590:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 2591:	**==========================================================================*/
 2592:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_LEFT_action(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP, UINT8 p_menu_action_U8)
 2593:	{
 2594:	  MENU_CB_RESULT_T          fl_cb_result_e;
 2595:	  UINT8                     fl_item_entry_type_U8;
 2596:	  UINT16                    fl_entry_array_idx_U16;
 2597:	  MENU_DEF_T  const        *fl_menu_def_SP;
 2598:	  UINT16                    fl_item_properties_U16;
 2599:	  BOOLEAN                   fl_disp_update_BOOL;
 2600:	  MENU_ANIM_DATA_T          fl_menu_anim_data;
 2601:	
 2602:	  fl_cb_result_e        = MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE;
 2603:	  fl_disp_update_BOOL   = FALSE;
 2604:	
 2605:	  if(p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)
 2606:	  {
 2607:	    fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 2608:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 2609:	    {
 2610:	      /*For dynamic menu, number of entries may be more than HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS. In this case,
 2611:	      fl_entry_array_idx_U16 will contains actual index instead of absolute index. Also if 
 2612:	      'is_menu_item_enable_cb' is null, then the index value will be taken from focus_line_U16*/
 2613:	      #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2614:	         if((fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL) && (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS))
 2615:	         {
 2616:	           /* hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry is not used here intentionally since focus_line_U16 is  set to
 2617:	           ** fl_entry_array_idx_U16 if p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 > HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS
 2618:	           */
 2619:	           fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_itm_absl_indx[p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16];
 2620:	           /* clear Gray out item bit */
 2621:	           CLEARBIT(fl_entry_array_idx_U16, BIT7);
 2622:	         }
 2623:	         else
 2624:	         {
 2625:	           fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2626:	         }
 2627:	      #else
 2628:	         fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2629:	      #endif
 2630:	    }
 2631:	    else
 2632:	    {
 2633:	      if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_CLOCK_FORMAT) != 0)
 2634:	      {
 2635:	        fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = 0;
 2636:	      }
 2637:	      else
 2638:	      {
 2639:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2640:	        fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2641:	        /* clear Gray out item bit */
 2642:	        CLEARBIT(fl_entry_array_idx_U16, BIT7);
 2643:	#else
 2644:	        fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2645:	#endif
 2646:	      }
 2647:	    }
 2648:	    fl_item_properties_U16 = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].item_properties;
 2649:	    fl_item_entry_type_U8 = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].item_entry_type;
 2650:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16  = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2651:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E       = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 2652:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2653:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8    = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2654:	
 2655:	    switch (fl_item_entry_type_U8)
 2656:	    {
 2657:	    case MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_BARGRAPH:
 2658:	      {
 2659:	        if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_CLOCK_FORMAT) != 0)
 2660:	        {
 2661:	          if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 == 0)
 2662:	          {
 2663:	            if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND) != 0)
 2664:	            {
 2665:	              p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr;
 2666:	            }
 2667:	          }
 2668:	          else
 2669:	          {
 2670:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16--;
 2671:	          }
 2672:	        }
 2673:	        if((fl_menu_def_SP->cb != FNULL) && \
 2674:	          ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_CLOCK_FORMAT) == 0))
 2675:	        {
 2676:	          if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_NORMAL)
 2677:	          {
 2678:	            (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_DECREMENT, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2679:	          }
 2680:	          else
 2681:	          {
 2682:	            (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_DECREMENT_FAST, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2683:	          }
 2684:	          fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_SCALE_ADJ_LEFT_ANM;
 2685:	          fl_disp_update_BOOL = TRUE;
 2686:	        }
 2687:	      }
 2688:	      break;
 2689:	    default:
 2690:	      {
 2691:	        if((fl_item_properties_U16 & MENU_ITEM_PRO_ACTION_EXIT) != 0)
 2692:	        {
 2693:	           fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT;
 2694:	        }
 2695:	        else
 2696:	        {
 2697:	           /* irrespective of left/left_long action, for item property BACK open parent menu */
 2698:	           if((fl_item_properties_U16 & MENU_ITEM_PRO_ACTION_BACK) != 0)
 2699:	           {
 2700:	              p_menu_action_U8 = MENU_ACTION_LEFT;
 2701:	           }
 2702:	           if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_level_U8 != HMI_MENU_LEVEL_0)
 2703:	           {
 2704:	              fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_left_menu_action(p_menu_cntrl_SP, p_menu_action_U8);
 2705:	              fl_disp_update_BOOL = TRUE;
 2706:	           }
 2707:	        }
 2708:	      }
 2709:	      break; /* End of default */
 2710:	    }
 2711:	
 2712:	    if(fl_disp_update_BOOL != FALSE)
 2713:	    {
 2714:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2715:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 2716:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2717:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2718:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 2719:	      #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 2720:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 2721:	      (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 2722:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 2723:	      fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 2724:	      #else
 2725:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 2726:	      fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 2727:	      #endif
 2728:	#else
 2729:	      if(MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT == fl_cb_result_e)
 2730:	      {
 2731:	        hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 2732:	      }
 2733:	      else
 2734:	      {
 2735:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 2736:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 2737:	                    , ANIM_MAX_ID
 2738:	#endif
 2739:	                    , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 2740:	      }
 2741:	#endif
 2742:	    }
 2743:	  }
 2744:	  return (fl_cb_result_e);
 2745:	}
 2746:	/*============================================================================
 2747:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_RIGHT_action
 2748:	** Visibility:       global
 2749:	** Description:      This function process the Menu Right Action.
 2750:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 2751:	                        the Action.
 2752:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 2753:	                        corresponding states.
 2754:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 2755:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2756:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 2757:	** Critical Section: None.
 2758:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 2759:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 2760:	**==========================================================================*/
 2761:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_RIGHT_action(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 2762:	{
 2763:	  MENU_CB_RESULT_T            fl_cb_result_e;
 2764:	  UINT8                       fl_get_menu_inst_U8;
 2765:	  UINT16                      fl_entry_array_idx_U16;
 2766:	  const MENU_DEF_T           *fl_sub_menu_def_ptr;
 2767:	  MENU_DEF_T  const          *fl_menu_def_SP;
 2768:	  MENU_ANIM_DATA_T            fl_menu_anim_data;
 2769:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2770:	  BOOLEAN                     fl_itm_gray_out_BOOL;
 2771:	#endif
 2772:	  
 2773:	  fl_cb_result_e       = MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE;
 2774:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2775:	  fl_itm_gray_out_BOOL = FALSE;
 2776:	#endif
 2777:	
 2778:	  if(p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)
 2779:	  {
 2780:	    fl_get_menu_inst_U8  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 2781:	  }
 2782:	  if((p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)&&(fl_get_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 2783:	  {
 2784:	    fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 2785:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 2786:	    {
 2787:	      /*For dynamic menu, number of entries may be more than HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS. In this case,
 2788:	      fl_entry_array_idx_U16 will contains actual index instead of absolute index. Also if 
 2789:	      'is_menu_item_enable_cb' is null, then the index value will be taken from focus_line_U16*/
 2790:	      #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2791:	         if((fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL) && (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS))
 2792:	         {
 2793:	           /* hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry is not used here intentionally since focus_line_U16 is  set to
 2794:	           ** fl_entry_array_idx_U16 if p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 > HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS
 2795:	           */
 2796:	           fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_itm_absl_indx[p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16];
 2797:	         }
 2798:	         else
 2799:	         {
 2800:	           fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2801:	         }
 2802:	      #else
 2803:	         fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2804:	      #endif
 2805:	    }
 2806:	    else
 2807:	    {
 2808:	      if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_CLOCK_FORMAT) != 0)
 2809:	      {
 2810:	        fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = 0;
 2811:	      }
 2812:	      else
 2813:	      {
 2814:	        #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2815:	           fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2816:	        #else
 2817:	           fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2818:	        #endif
 2819:	      }
 2820:	    }
 2821:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2822:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 2823:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2824:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2825:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2826:	    /* Item Gray out check - No action on it */
 2827:	    if((TESTBIT(fl_entry_array_idx_U16, BIT7)) && ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC))
 2828:	    {
 2829:	      fl_itm_gray_out_BOOL = TRUE;
 2830:	    }
 2831:	    if(fl_itm_gray_out_BOOL == FALSE)
 2832:	    {
 2833:	#endif
 2834:	      if ((fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].item_properties & MENU_ITEM_PRO_DISABLED) == 0)
 2835:	      {
 2836:	        switch (fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].item_entry_type)
 2837:	        {
 2838:	        case MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_MENU:
 2839:	          fl_sub_menu_def_ptr = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].utype.sub_menu;
 2840:	          /* Call callback */
 2841:	          if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 2842:	          {
 2843:	            fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_RIGHT, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2844:	          }
 2845:	          /* Sub menu */
 2846:	          if (fl_sub_menu_def_ptr != FNULL)
 2847:	          {
 2848:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(fl_sub_menu_def_ptr, fl_menu_def_SP, fl_get_menu_inst_U8, MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_PARENT);
 2849:	            #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 2850:	              if(fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)
 2851:	              {
 2852:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_RIGHT_ANM;
 2853:	                      #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 2854:	                      MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 2855:	                      #endif
 2856:	              }
 2857:	              else
 2858:	              {
 2859:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_RIGHT_WAIT_ANM;
 2860:	              }
 2861:	            #endif
 2862:	          }
 2863:	          break;
 2864:	
 2865:	        case MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_BARGRAPH:
 2866:	          if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_CLOCK_FORMAT) != 0)
 2867:	          {
 2868:	                            if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 + 1) == p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr)
 2869:	              {
 2870:	                  if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_FOCUS_WRAPAROUND)!= 0)
 2871:	                  {
 2872:	                      p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = 0;
 2873:	                  }
 2874:	              }
 2875:	                            else if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 < p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr)
 2876:	              {
 2877:	                p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16++;
 2878:	              }
 2879:	                            else
 2880:	                            {
 2881:	                                /*Do Nothing*/
 2882:	            }
 2883:	          }
 2884:	          if((fl_menu_def_SP->cb != FNULL) && \
 2885:	            ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_CLOCK_FORMAT) == 0))
 2886:	          {
 2887:	            if(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_state == MENU_STATE_NORMAL)
 2888:	            {
 2889:	              (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_INCREMENT, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2890:	            }
 2891:	            else
 2892:	            {
 2893:	              (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_INCREMENT_FAST, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 2894:	            }
 2895:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_SCALE_ADJ_RIGHT_ANM;
 2896:	          }
 2897:	          break;
 2898:	
 2899:	        default:
 2900:	          break;
 2901:	        }
 2902:	      }
 2903:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2904:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 2905:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 2906:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 2907:	  #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 2908:	      #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 2909:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 2910:	      (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 2911:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 2912:	      fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 2913:	      #else
 2914:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 2915:	      fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 2916:	      #endif    
 2917:	  #else
 2918:	      if(MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT == fl_cb_result_e)
 2919:	      {
 2920:	        hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 2921:	      }
 2922:	      else
 2923:	      {
 2924:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 2925:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 2926:	                    , ANIM_MAX_ID
 2927:	#endif
 2928:	                    , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 2929:	      }
 2930:	  #endif
 2931:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2932:	    }
 2933:	#endif
 2934:	  }
 2935:	  return (fl_cb_result_e);
 2936:	}
 2937:	/*============================================================================
 2938:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_OK_action
 2939:	** Visibility:       global
 2940:	** Description:      This function process the Menu Ok Action.
 2941:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 2942:	                        the Action.
 2943:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 2944:	                        corresponding states.
 2945:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 2946:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 2947:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 2948:	** Critical Section: None.
 2949:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 2950:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 2951:	**==========================================================================*/
 2952:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_OK_action(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP, UINT8 p_menu_action_U8)
 2953:	{
 2954:	  MENU_CB_RESULT_T      fl_cb_result_e;
 2955:	  MENU_LIST_ID_T        fl_get_menu_ID_e;
 2956:	  UINT8                 fl_get_menu_inst_U8;
 2957:	  UINT8                 fl_get_entry_type_U8;
 2958:	  UINT16                fl_entry_array_idx_U16;
 2959:	  UINT16                fl_item_properties_U16;
 2960:	  const MENU_DEF_T     *fl_sub_menu_ptr;
 2961:	  MENU_DEF_T  const    *fl_menu_def_SP;
 2962:	  MENU_ANIM_DATA_T      fl_menu_anim_data;
 2963:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 2964:	  BOOLEAN               fl_menu_close_anim_BOOL;
 2965:	#endif
 2966:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2967:	  BOOLEAN               fl_itm_gray_out_BOOL;
 2968:	#endif
 2969:	  fl_cb_result_e          = MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE;
 2970:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 2971:	  fl_menu_close_anim_BOOL = FALSE;
 2972:	#endif
 2973:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2974:	  fl_itm_gray_out_BOOL = FALSE;
 2975:	#endif
 2976:	  if(p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)
 2977:	  {
 2978:	    fl_get_menu_inst_U8  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 2979:	  }
 2980:	  if((p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)&&(fl_get_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 2981:	  {
 2982:	    fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 2983:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 2984:	    {
 2985:	      /*For dynamic menu, number of entries may be more than HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS. In this case,
 2986:	      fl_entry_array_idx_U16 will contains actual index instead of absolute index. Also if 
 2987:	      'is_menu_item_enable_cb' is null, then the index value will be taken from focus_line_U16*/
 2988:	      #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 2989:	         if((fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL) && (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS))
 2990:	         {
 2991:	           /* hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry is not used here intentionally since focus_line_U16 is  set to
 2992:	           ** fl_entry_array_idx_U16 if p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 > HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS
 2993:	           */
 2994:	           fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_itm_absl_indx[p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16];
 2995:	         }
 2996:	         else
 2997:	         {
 2998:	            fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 2999:	         }
 3000:	      #else
 3001:	         fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 3002:	      #endif
 3003:	    }
 3004:	    else
 3005:	    {
 3006:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3007:	      fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3008:	#else
 3009:	      fl_entry_array_idx_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 3010:	#endif
 3011:	    }
 3012:	
 3013:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3014:	    /* Item Gray out check - No action on it */
 3015:	    if((TESTBIT(fl_entry_array_idx_U16, BIT7)) && ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC))
 3016:	    {
 3017:	      fl_itm_gray_out_BOOL = TRUE;
 3018:	    }
 3019:	    if(fl_itm_gray_out_BOOL == FALSE)
 3020:	    {
 3021:	#endif
 3022:	      fl_item_properties_U16 = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].item_properties;
 3023:	      fl_get_entry_type_U8 = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].item_entry_type;
 3024:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 3025:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 3026:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 3027:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3028:	      switch (fl_get_entry_type_U8)
 3029:	      {
 3030:	      case MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_MENU:
 3031:	        if ((fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].item_properties & MENU_ITEM_PRO_DISABLED) == 0)
 3032:	        {
 3033:	          fl_sub_menu_ptr = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].utype.sub_menu;
 3034:	
 3035:	          if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DISABLE_SELECTED_ENTRY) == 0)
 3036:	          {
 3037:	  #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3038:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->selected_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3039:	  #else
 3040:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->selected_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 3041:	  #endif
 3042:	          }
 3043:	
 3044:	          /* Call callback */
 3045:	          if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 3046:	          {
 3047:	            fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb((MENU_ACTION_T)p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 3048:	          }
 3049:	          /* Sub menu */
 3050:	
 3051:	  #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3052:	          if(TESTBIT(fl_cb_result_e, HMI_MENU_EXIT_WITH_ANIM_MASK))
 3053:	          {
 3054:	            fl_menu_close_anim_BOOL = TRUE;
 3055:	          }
 3056:	          if ((fl_sub_menu_ptr != FNULL) &&
 3057:	               (fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT) &&
 3058:	                (fl_menu_close_anim_BOOL == FALSE))
 3059:	  #else
 3060:	            if ((fl_sub_menu_ptr != FNULL) && (fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT))
 3061:	  #endif
 3062:	              {
 3063:	              fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(fl_sub_menu_ptr, fl_menu_def_SP, fl_get_menu_inst_U8, MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_PARENT);
 3064:	  #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3065:	              if(fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)
 3066:	              {
 3067:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_RIGHT_ANM;
 3068:	                #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 3069:	                 MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 3070:	                #endif
 3071:	              }
 3072:	              else
 3073:	              {
 3074:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_RIGHT_WAIT_ANM;
 3075:	              }
 3076:	  #endif
 3077:	            }
 3078:	        }
 3079:	        break;
 3080:	
 3081:	      case MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_BARGRAPH:
 3082:	        if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 3083:	        {
 3084:	          fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb((MENU_ACTION_T)p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 3085:	        }
 3086:	  #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3087:	        if(TESTBIT(fl_cb_result_e, HMI_MENU_EXIT_WITH_ANIM_MASK))
 3088:	        {
 3089:	          fl_menu_close_anim_BOOL = TRUE;
 3090:	        }
 3091:	        if((fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT) && (fl_menu_close_anim_BOOL == FALSE))
 3092:	  #else
 3093:	          if (fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT)
 3094:	  #endif
 3095:	          {
 3096:	            /* Root menu => Nothing to do */
 3097:	            fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT;
 3098:	          }
 3099:	          break;
 3100:	      case MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_LABEL:
 3101:	      case MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_EXE:
 3102:	      case MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_TOGGLE:
 3103:	        if((fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].item_properties  & MENU_ITEM_PRO_DISABLED) == 0)
 3104:	        {
 3105:	          if(fl_get_entry_type_U8 == MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_TOGGLE)
 3106:	          {
 3107:	            if(*(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id].entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].utype.toggle) == TRUE)
 3108:	            {
 3109:	              *(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id].entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].utype.toggle) = FALSE;
 3110:	            }
 3111:	            else
 3112:	            {
 3113:	              *(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id].entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].utype.toggle) = TRUE;
 3114:	            }
 3115:	          }
 3116:	          if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DISABLE_SELECTED_ENTRY) == 0)
 3117:	          {
 3118:	  #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3119:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->selected_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3120:	  #else
 3121:	            p_menu_cntrl_SP->selected_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 3122:	  #endif
 3123:	          }
 3124:	          /* Open the Menu Instance */
 3125:	          if((fl_get_entry_type_U8 == MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_EXE) &&
 3126:	              (p_menu_action_U8 == MENU_ACTION_OK_IN) &&
 3127:	               (fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].utype.sub_menu != NULL))
 3128:	          {
 3129:	            fl_get_menu_ID_e = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_index(fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_entry_array_idx_U16].utype.sub_menu);
 3130:	            MENU_CORE_MENU_OPEN(fl_get_menu_ID_e, HMI_MENU_INSTANCE_OPEN_ANIM);
 3131:	          }
 3132:	          /* Call callback */
 3133:	          if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 3134:	          {
 3135:	            fl_cb_result_e = fl_menu_def_SP->cb((MENU_ACTION_T)p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 3136:	          }
 3137:	          
 3138:	          if(fl_get_entry_type_U8 == MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_LABEL)
 3139:	          {
 3140:	             /* for item entry property BACK, open parent menu */
 3141:	             if((fl_item_properties_U16 & MENU_ITEM_PRO_ACTION_BACK) != 0)
 3142:	             {
 3143:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_left_menu_action(p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_ACTION_LEFT);
 3144:	             }
 3145:	             else if((fl_item_properties_U16 & MENU_ITEM_PRO_ACTION_EXIT) != 0)
 3146:	             {
 3147:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT;
 3148:	             }
 3149:	             else
 3150:	                            {
 3151:	                            }
 3152:	          }
 3153:	          
 3154:	          /* Since in the EXE type of Menu, Ok press shall always open the new Menu
 3155:	             there is no need to refresh the Menu */
 3156:	          if(fl_get_entry_type_U8 != MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_EXE)
 3157:	          {
 3158:	            hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Refresh(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 3159:	          }
 3160:	        }
 3161:	        break;
 3162:	      default:
 3163:	        break;
 3164:	      }
 3165:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16;
 3166:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id;
 3167:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 3168:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3169:	  #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3170:	      #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 3171:	      if(fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E != fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E)
 3172:	      {
 3173:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 3174:	        (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3175:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 3176:	        fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3177:	      }
 3178:	      else
 3179:	      {
 3180:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 3181:	        fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3182:	      }
 3183:	      #else
 3184:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 3185:	      fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3186:	      #endif    
 3187:	  #else
 3188:	      if(MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT == fl_cb_result_e)
 3189:	      {
 3190:	        hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 3191:	      }
 3192:	      else
 3193:	      {
 3194:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 3195:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3196:	                    , ANIM_MAX_ID
 3197:	#endif
 3198:	                    , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 3199:	      }
 3200:	  #endif
 3201:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3202:	    }
 3203:	#endif
 3204:	  }
 3205:	  return (fl_cb_result_e);
 3206:	}
 3207:	/*============================================================================
 3208:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Other_action
 3209:	** Visibility:       global
 3210:	** Description:      This function process all the Menu Action other than
 3211:	                     LEFT/RIGHT/UP/DOWN.
 3212:	                     1. It calls the specific component CallBack and indicate
 3213:	                        the Action.
 3214:	                     2. Holds the return value to settle down in the
 3215:	                        corresponding states.
 3216:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 3217:	** Inputs/:          p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP
 3218:	** Outputs           fl_cb_result_e
 3219:	** Critical Section: None.
 3220:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3221:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3222:	**==========================================================================*/
 3223:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_Other_action(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP, UINT8 p_menu_action_U8)
 3224:	{
 3225:	  MENU_CB_RESULT_T       fl_cb_result_e;
 3226:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3227:	  UINT8                  p_menu_anim_ID_U8;
 3228:	#endif
 3229:	  fl_cb_result_e       = MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE;
 3230:	  if (hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id].cb != NULL)
 3231:	  {
 3232:	    fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id].cb((MENU_ACTION_T)p_menu_action_U8, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 3233:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3234:	    if((fl_cb_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT)||(TESTBIT(fl_cb_result_e, HMI_MENU_EXIT_WITH_ANIM_MASK)))
 3235:	    {
 3236:	      /*fl_cb_result_e contains 16 bit result. Here we are extracting the first 8 bit data
 3237:	      to get the animation id*/
 3238:	      p_menu_anim_ID_U8 = (fl_cb_result_e & HMI_MENU_CB_CLOSE_ANIM_MASK);
 3239:	      hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, p_menu_anim_ID_U8);
 3240:	    }
 3241:	#else
 3242:	    if (fl_cb_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT)
 3243:	    {
 3244:	      hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 3245:	    }
 3246:	#endif
 3247:	    else
 3248:	    {
 3249:	      switch(fl_cb_result_e)
 3250:	      {
 3251:	      case MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE:
 3252:	        break;
 3253:	      case MENU_CB_ACTION_DOWN:
 3254:	                    fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_DOWN_action(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3255:	        break;
 3256:	      case MENU_CB_ACTION_UP:
 3257:	                    fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_UP_action(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3258:	        break;
 3259:	      case MENU_CB_ACTION_RIGHT:
 3260:	                    fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_RIGHT_action(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3261:	        break;
 3262:	      case MENU_CB_ACTION_LEFT:
 3263:	        fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_LEFT_action(p_menu_cntrl_SP, p_menu_action_U8);
 3264:	        break;
 3265:	      case MENU_CB_ACTION_OK:
 3266:	        fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_OK_action(p_menu_cntrl_SP, p_menu_action_U8);
 3267:	        break;
 3268:	      default:
 3269:	        break;
 3270:	      }
 3271:	    }
 3272:	  }
 3273:	  return(fl_cb_result_e);
 3274:	}
 3275:	/*============================================================================
 3276:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Refresh
 3277:	** Visibility:       global
 3278:	** Description:      This function triggers the presentation from Menu
 3279:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 3280:	** Inputs:           p_menu_pre_state_U8 - MENU_PRE_ADD_STATE
 3281:	                                         - MENU_PRE_REMOVE_STATE
 3282:	** Critical Section: None.
 3283:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3284:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3285:	**==========================================================================*/
 3286:	void hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Refresh(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8)
 3287:	{
 3288:	  MENU_CONTROL_T *fl_menu_rfrsh_ID_ptr;
 3289:	  LSH_STATE_ID_T fl_menu_screen_id_U8;
 3290:	
 3291:	  fl_menu_rfrsh_ID_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_menu_id_U8);
 3292:	  if(fl_menu_rfrsh_ID_ptr != FNULL)
 3293:	  {
 3294:	    fl_menu_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_rfrsh_ID_ptr);
 3295:	        hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(p_menu_id_U8, fl_menu_screen_id_U8
 3296:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3297:	            , ANIM_MAX_ID
 3298:	#endif
 3299:	            , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 3300:	  }
 3301:	}
 3302:	/*============================================================================
 3303:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Rebuild
 3304:	** Visibility:       global
 3305:	** Description:      This function triggers the reloading of MEnu contents
 3306:	                     and finally triggers the presentation.
 3307:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 3308:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_id_U8
 3309:	** Critical Section: None.
 3310:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3311:	** Updated:          01-Dec-2013 by csakthiv
 3312:	**==========================================================================*/
 3313:	void hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Rebuild(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8)
 3314:	{
 3315:	    MENU_CONTROL_T        *fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr;
 3316:	    UINT8                  fl_get_menu_inst_U8;
 3317:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T       fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 3318:	    UINT16                 fl_store_focused_entry_U16;
 3319:	    MENU_ANIM_DATA_T       fl_menu_anim_data;
 3320:	    #if(HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_REBUILD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_INCR_TILL_VALID) || (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_REBUILD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_DECR_TILL_VALID)
 3321:	    UINT16                 fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16;
 3322:	    #endif
 3323:	    fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_menu_id_U8);
 3324:	
 3325:	    if(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr != FNULL)
 3326:	    {
 3327:	        fl_get_menu_inst_U8    = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_menu_id_U8);
 3328:	        if(fl_get_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 3329:	        {
 3330:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 3331:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E = fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_id;
 3332:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 3333:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr);
 3334:	
 3335:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3336:	                fl_store_focused_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr);;
 3337:	                CLEARBIT(fl_store_focused_entry_U16, BIT7);
 3338:	            #endif
 3339:	
 3340:	
 3341:	            hmi_menu_logic_create_menu(p_menu_id_U8, fl_get_menu_inst_U8);
 3342:	
 3343:	            if(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_id].cb != FNULL)
 3344:	            {
 3345:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_id].cb(MENU_ACTION_REBUILD,\
 3346:	                  fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 3347:	            }
 3348:	            #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3349:	                if(fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)
 3350:	                {
 3351:	                    fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_REBUILD_ANM;
 3352:	                }
 3353:	                else
 3354:	                {
 3355:	                    /* menu chart will enter into wait state*/
 3356:	                }
 3357:	            #endif
 3358:	
 3359:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3360:	                /* check is made to ensure that the stored item is present after rebuild.
 3361:	                Also if the item is not present then the last saved itm value is updated 
 3362:	                inside the hmi_menu_app_get_stored_menu_item() and the default value is considered */
 3363:	                #ifdef HMI_MENU_HISTORY_HANDLING_FEATURE
 3364:	                    if((fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_HISTORY_MENU) != 0)
 3365:	                    {
 3366:	                        fl_store_focused_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_app_get_stored_menu_item(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_id);
 3367:	                    }
 3368:	                #endif
 3369:	                /* Should not be a Dynamic Menu */
 3370:	                if((fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) == 0)
 3371:	                {
 3372:	                    hmi_menu_logic_set_valid_focus_entry(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr, fl_store_focused_entry_U16);
 3373:	                }
 3374:	
 3375:	                /*Here we are checking the 'is_menu_item_enable_cb != null' validation to ensure that absolute
 3376:	                index is updated for dynamic menu.*/
 3377:	                /*if static || (dynamic && is_menu_item_enable_cb != null && focus_entry < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS) */
 3378:	                if(((fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) == 0) ||
 3379:	                    ((hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_id].is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL) &&
 3380:	                    (fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->focus_entry_U16 < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS)))
 3381:	                {
 3382:	                    #if (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_REBUILD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_INCR_TILL_VALID)
 3383:	                        fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr);
 3384:	                        if(TESTBIT(fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16,BIT7))
 3385:	                        {
 3386:	                            /* on reload's rebuild if the current focus entry is greyout, then increment fe till valid fe */
 3387:	                            hmi_menu_logic_down_focus_entry(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr);
 3388:	                        }
 3389:	                    #elif (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_REBUILD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_DECR_TILL_VALID)
 3390:	                        fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr);
 3391:	                        if(TESTBIT(fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16,BIT7))
 3392:	                        {
 3393:	                            /* on reload's rebuild if the current focus entry is greyout, then decrement fe till valid fe */
 3394:	                            hmi_menu_logic_up_focus_entry(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr);
 3395:	                        }
 3396:	                    #else
 3397:	                        /* do nothing.keep the focus entry in the greyed out item itself */
 3398:	                    #endif
 3399:	                }
 3400:	
 3401:	                if ((fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) == 0)
 3402:	                {
 3403:	                    hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr);
 3404:	                }
 3405:	            #endif
 3406:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 3407:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E = fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->menu_id;
 3408:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 3409:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_inst_ptr);
 3410:	
 3411:	            #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3412:	                #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 3413:	                    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 3414:	                    (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3415:	                    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 3416:	                    fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3417:	                #else
 3418:	                    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 3419:	                    fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3420:	                #endif
 3421:	            #else
 3422:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(p_menu_id_U8, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 3423:	                    #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3424:	                        , ANIM_MAX_ID
 3425:	                    #endif
 3426:	                    , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 3427:	            #endif
 3428:	            /*Update the callback result to menu core chart like animation,please wait or completed*/
 3429:	            hmi_menu_chart_menu_rebuild(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_get_menu_inst_U8],fl_cb_result_e);
 3430:	        }
 3431:	    }
 3432:	}
 3433:	/*============================================================================
 3434:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Menu_ReinitAction
 3435:	** Visibility:       global
 3436:	** Description:      Reinitialization Actions are performed.
 3437:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 3438:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_cntrl_SP
 3439:	** Critical Section: None.
 3440:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3441:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3442:	**==========================================================================*/
 3443:	MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_Menu_ReinitAction(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 3444:	{
 3445:	  MENU_CB_RESULT_T       fl_cb_result_e;
 3446:	  fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_Other_action(p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_ACTION_REINIT);
 3447:	  return(fl_cb_result_e);
 3448:	}
 3449:	/*============================================================================
 3450:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_MenuDataAvailable
 3451:	** Visibility:       global
 3452:	** Description:      After the Data is recieved then the Menu Actions are done here
 3453:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Menu chart
 3454:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_id_U8
 3455:	** Critical Section: None.
 3456:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3457:	** Updated:          23-July-2014 by csakthiv
 3458:	**==========================================================================*/
 3459:	MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_MenuDataAvailable(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8)
 3460:	{
 3461:	  MENU_CONTROL_T        *fl_menu_cntrl_ptr;
 3462:	  MENU_CB_RESULT_T      fl_cb_result_e;
 3463:	  UINT8                 fl_menu_inst_idx_U8;
 3464:	  MENU_ANIM_DATA_T      fl_menu_anim_data;
 3465:	  
 3466:	  fl_cb_result_e      = MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE;
 3467:	
 3468:	  fl_menu_inst_idx_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_menu_id_U8);
 3469:	  fl_menu_cntrl_ptr   = hmi_menu_logic_get_required_menu_instance(fl_menu_inst_idx_U8);
 3470:	  
 3471:	  if((fl_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL) && (fl_menu_inst_idx_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 3472:	  {
 3473:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 3474:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E      = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id;
 3475:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 3476:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 3477:	
 3478:	    fl_menu_cntrl_ptr   = &l_menu_instance_array[fl_menu_inst_idx_U8]; 
 3479:	
 3480:	    if (hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].cb != NULL)
 3481:	    {
 3482:	      if(FALSE != l_end_of_fast_scroll_BOOL)
 3483:	      {
 3484:	        /*Once the End of list or Start of list reached during fast scroll on last
 3485:	          request response we need to Stop the fast scroll animation and change
 3486:	          the mode to normal to get the full display.*/
 3487:	        hmi_menu_fast_scroll_inactive(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_menu_inst_idx_U8]);
 3488:	        l_end_of_fast_scroll_BOOL = FALSE;
 3489:	      }
 3490:	      fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].cb(MENU_ACTION_DATA_AVAIL, fl_menu_cntrl_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 3491:	      #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3492:	        #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 3493:	            MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 3494:	        #endif
 3495:	      #endif
 3496:	    }
 3497:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 3498:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E       = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id;
 3499:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 3500:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8    = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 3501:	    #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3502:	    fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 3503:	    fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3504:	    #else
 3505:	    if(MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT == fl_cb_result_e)
 3506:	    {
 3507:	       hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 3508:	    }
 3509:	    else
 3510:	    {
 3511:	            hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 3512:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3513:	                , ANIM_MAX_ID
 3514:	#endif
 3515:	                , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 3516:	    }
 3517:	    #endif
 3518:	  }
 3519:	  return(fl_cb_result_e);
 3520:	}
 3521:	
 3522:	/*============================================================================
 3523:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_MenuCompletion
 3524:	** Visibility:       global
 3525:	** Description:      After completion of the Fast Scroll the Menu Actions are
 3526:	**                   handled to bring the display.
 3527:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 3528:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_def_ptr
 3529:	** Critical Section: None.
 3530:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3531:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3532:	**==========================================================================*/
 3533:	MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_MenuCompletion(
 3534:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_FAST_SCROLL_ENABLE
 3535:	    MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8
 3536:	#else
 3537:	    void
 3538:	#endif
 3539:	    )
 3540:	{
 3541:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T    fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE;
 3542:	
 3543:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_FAST_SCROLL_ENABLE
 3544:	    MENU_CONTROL_T      *fl_menu_cntrl_ptr;
 3545:	    MENU_ANIM_DATA_T      fl_menu_anim_data;
 3546:	
 3547:	    fl_menu_cntrl_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_menu_id_U8);
 3548:	
 3549:	    if (fl_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL)
 3550:	    {
 3551:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 3552:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E     = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id;
 3553:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 3554:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 3555:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 3556:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E     = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id;
 3557:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 3558:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8   = fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8;
 3559:	
 3560:	        if (hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].cb != NULL)
 3561:	        {
 3562:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].cb(MENU_ACTION_COMPLETION, fl_menu_cntrl_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 3563:	        }
 3564:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3565:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 3566:	        fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 3567:	#else
 3568:	        hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 3569:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 3570:	            , ANIM_MAX_ID
 3571:	#endif
 3572:	            , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 3573:	#endif
 3574:	    }
 3575:	#endif
 3576:	    return(fl_cb_result_e);
 3577:	}
 3578:	/*============================================================================
 3579:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Set_MenuState
 3580:	** Visibility:       global
 3581:	** Description:      After completion of the Fast Scroll the Menu Actions are
 3582:	**                   handled to bring the display.
 3583:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_chart_process_menu_action ()
 3584:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_def_ptr
 3585:	** Critical Section: None.
 3586:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3587:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3588:	**==========================================================================*/
 3589:	void hmi_menu_logic_Set_MenuState(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8,UINT8 p_menu_state_U8)
 3590:	{
 3591:	  MENU_CONTROL_T      *fl_menu_cntrl_ptr;
 3592:	  fl_menu_cntrl_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_menu_id_U8);
 3593:	  if(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL)
 3594:	  {
 3595:	     fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_state = p_menu_state_U8;
 3596:	  }
 3597:	}
 3598:	/*============================================================================
 3599:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_action
 3600:	** Visibility:       global
 3601:	** Description:      This function routes to the respective Menu Button function
 3602:	**                   handlers to process the button.
 3603:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_chart.c
 3604:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_action_U8
 3605:	** Critical Section: None.
 3606:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3607:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3608:	**==========================================================================*/
 3609:	MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_action(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8, UINT8 p_menu_action_U8)
 3610:	{
 3611:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T        fl_cb_result_e;
 3612:	    MENU_CONTROL_T         *fl_menu_cntrl_ptr;
 3613:	    fl_cb_result_e    = MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE;
 3614:	
 3615:	    fl_menu_cntrl_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_menu_id_U8);
 3616:	
 3617:	    if(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL)
 3618:	    {
 3619:	        switch (p_menu_action_U8)
 3620:	        {
 3621:	        case MENU_ACTION_UP:
 3622:	        case MENU_ACTION_UP_FAST:
 3623:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_UP_action(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 3624:	            break;
 3625:	        case MENU_ACTION_DOWN:
 3626:	        case MENU_ACTION_DOWN_FAST:
 3627:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_DOWN_action(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 3628:	            break;
 3629:	        case MENU_ACTION_LEFT:
 3630:	        case MENU_ACTION_LEFT_LONG:
 3631:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_LEFT_action(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr, p_menu_action_U8);
 3632:	            break;
 3633:	        case MENU_ACTION_RIGHT:
 3634:	        case MENU_ACTION_RIGHT_LONG:
 3635:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_RIGHT_action(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 3636:	            break;
 3637:	        case MENU_ACTION_OK_IN:
 3638:	        case MENU_ACTION_OK_LONG:
 3639:	        case MENU_ACTION_OK_RELEASE:
 3640:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_OK_action(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr, p_menu_action_U8);
 3641:	            break;
 3642:	        default:
 3643:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_Other_action(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr, p_menu_action_U8);
 3644:	            break;
 3645:	        }
 3646:	    }
 3647:	    return(fl_cb_result_e);
 3648:	}
 3649:	/*============================================================================
 3650:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Set_SelectEntry
 3651:	** Visibility:       global
 3652:	** Description:      This function is used to set the particular Menu item
 3653:	**                   as selected one. Application should call this interface 
 3654:	**                   in MENU_ACTION_OPEN so that both focus and selected entry will be same.
 3655:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 3656:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_def_ptr, p_entry_S16
 3657:	** Critical Section: None.
 3658:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3659:	** Updated:          24-Oct-2013 by csakthiv
 3660:	**==========================================================================*/
 3661:	void hmi_menu_logic_Set_SelectEntry(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP, UINT16 p_entry_S16)
 3662:	{
 3663:	  /* Selected value is loaded */
 3664:	  if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DISABLE_SELECTED_ENTRY) == 0)
 3665:	  {
 3666:	    p_menu_cntrl_SP->selected_entry_U16 = p_entry_S16;
 3667:	  }
 3668:	  /* Validate and update the selected and focus entry value */
 3669:	  if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->selected_entry_U16 == MENU_INVALID_ENTRY)
 3670:	  {
 3671:	    /* Set focus on the first entry */
 3672:	    p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 3673:	  }
 3674:	  else
 3675:	  {
 3676:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DISABLE_SELECTED_ENTRY) == 0)
 3677:	    {
 3678:	       #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3679:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP,p_menu_cntrl_SP->selected_entry_U16);
 3680:	       #else
 3681:	         p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->selected_entry_U16; 
 3682:	       #endif
 3683:	      }
 3684:	  }
 3685:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_HISTORY_HANDLING_FEATURE
 3686:	  #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3687:	     HMI_MENU_HISTORY_STORE_MENU_ITEM(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_SP));
 3688:	  #else
 3689:	     HMI_MENU_HISTORY_STORE_MENU_ITEM(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id, p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16);
 3690:	  #endif
 3691:	#endif
 3692:	  /* To make the focus line on the center of the display */
 3693:	  hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3694:	}
 3695:	/*============================================================================
 3696:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center
 3697:	** Visibility:       global
 3698:	** Description:      Focus line position handling while opening the Menu
 3699:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 3700:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_cntrl_SP
 3701:	** Critical Section: None.
 3702:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3703:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3704:	**==========================================================================*/
 3705:	void hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP)
 3706:	{
 3707:	  if ((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 < (p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines >> 1)) || \
 3708:	    (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr <= p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines))
 3709:	  {
 3710:	    p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16;
 3711:	  }
 3712:	  else
 3713:	  {
 3714:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CENTRE_FOCUS_LINE
 3715:	    /* Used in L538,TVM and P552 */
 3716:	    if (p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 >= (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr - (p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines >> 1)))
 3717:	    {
 3718:	      p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 - (p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr - p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines);
 3719:	    }
 3720:	    else
 3721:	    {
 3722:	      p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = ((UINT16)(p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines - 1)) >> 1;
 3723:	    }
 3724:	#else
 3725:	    /* Used in P42 */
 3726:	    p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_line_U16 = ((UINT16)((p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16) % (p_menu_cntrl_SP->displayed_lines))) ;
 3727:	#endif
 3728:	
 3729:	  }
 3730:	}
 3731:	/*============================================================================
 3732:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_set_valid_focus_entry
 3733:	** Visibility:       global
 3734:	** Description:      This function is used for validating the stored focus entry
 3735:	**                   and assigning the proper value.
 3736:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Menu core
 3737:	** Inputs/Outputs:   None
 3738:	** Critical Section: None.
 3739:	** Created:          03-May-2010 by jpradeep
 3740:	** Updated:          03-May-2010 by jpradeep
 3741:	**==========================================================================*/
 3742:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3743:	static void hmi_menu_logic_set_valid_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP, UINT16 p_store_focused_entry_U16)
 3744:	{
 3745:	  UINT8 i;
 3746:	  UINT8 fl_get_absl_indx_val_U8;
 3747:	  BOOLEAN fl_item_config_BOOL;
 3748:	
 3749:	  fl_item_config_BOOL = FALSE;
 3750:	  CLEARBIT(p_store_focused_entry_U16, BIT7); /*clear gray out bit*/
 3751:	
 3752:	  if(p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)
 3753:	  {
 3754:	    for(i=0; i<p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_entry_nbr; i++)
 3755:	    {
 3756:	      fl_get_absl_indx_val_U8 = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_itm_absl_indx[i];
 3757:	      CLEARBIT(fl_get_absl_indx_val_U8, BIT7); /*clear gray out bit*/
 3758:	      if(fl_get_absl_indx_val_U8 == p_store_focused_entry_U16)
 3759:	      {
 3760:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = i;
 3761:	        fl_item_config_BOOL = TRUE;
 3762:	        break;
 3763:	      }
 3764:	    }
 3765:	    if(fl_item_config_BOOL == FALSE)
 3766:	    {
 3767:	        p_menu_cntrl_SP->focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 3768:	    }
 3769:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_HISTORY_HANDLING_FEATURE
 3770:	  hmi_menu_logic_update_history_info(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 3771:	#endif
 3772:	  }
 3773:	}
 3774:	#endif
 3775:	/*============================================================================
 3776:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_CreateLabel_Items
 3777:	** Visibility:       global
 3778:	** Description:      This function is used for quick Creation of the
 3779:	**                   Menu items with similar type - Label
 3780:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 3781:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_item_entry_ptr
 3782:	                     p_nb_entries_U16
 3783:	** Critical Section: None.
 3784:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3785:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3786:	**==========================================================================*/
 3787:	void hmi_menu_logic_CreateLabel_Items(MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_T *p_item_entry_ptr, UINT16 p_nb_entries_U16)
 3788:	{
 3789:	  UINT16 ind;
 3790:	  for (ind = 0 ; ind < p_nb_entries_U16; ind++)
 3791:	  {
 3792:	    p_item_entry_ptr[ind].item_entry_type = MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_LABEL;
 3793:	    p_item_entry_ptr[ind].item_properties = MENU_ITEM_PRO_LABEL_ID;
 3794:	    p_item_entry_ptr[ind].item_symbol     = 0;
 3795:	    p_item_entry_ptr[ind].utype.sub_menu  = FNULL;
 3796:	  }
 3797:	}
 3798:	/*============================================================================
 3799:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_CreateMenu_Items
 3800:	** Visibility:       global
 3801:	** Description:      This function is used for quick Creation of the
 3802:	**                   Menu items with similar type - Menu
 3803:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 3804:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_item_entry_ptr
 3805:	                     p_nb_entries_U16
 3806:	** Critical Section: None.
 3807:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3808:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3809:	**==========================================================================*/
 3810:	void hmi_menu_logic_CreateMenu_Items(MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_T *p_item_entry_ptr, UINT16 p_nb_entries_U16)
 3811:	{
 3812:	  UINT16 ind;
 3813:	  for (ind = 0 ; ind < p_nb_entries_U16; ind++)
 3814:	  {
 3815:	    p_item_entry_ptr[ind].item_entry_type = MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_MENU;
 3816:	    p_item_entry_ptr[ind].item_properties = 0;
 3817:	    p_item_entry_ptr[ind].utype.sub_menu  = FNULL;
 3818:	  }
 3819:	}
 3820:	/*============================================================================
 3821:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_MenuIsOpen
 3822:	** Visibility:       global
 3823:	** Description:      This function Checks whether the Requested Menu is open or not
 3824:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 3825:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_id_U8
 3826:	** Critical Section: None.
 3827:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3828:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3829:	**==========================================================================*/
 3830:	BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_MenuIsOpen(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8)
 3831:	{
 3832:	  BOOLEAN         fl_status_BOOL = FALSE;
 3833:	  MENU_CONTROL_T   *fl_menu_inst_ID_ptr;
 3834:	
 3835:	  fl_menu_inst_ID_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_menu_id_U8);
 3836:	  if(fl_menu_inst_ID_ptr != FNULL)
 3837:	  {
 3838:	    fl_status_BOOL = TRUE;
 3839:	  }
 3840:	  return(fl_status_BOOL);
 3841:	}
 3842:	/*============================================================================
 3843:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Get_MenuTimeOut
 3844:	** Visibility:       global
 3845:	** Description:      This function returns the Menu Timeout value
 3846:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 3847:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_id_U8
 3848:	** Critical Section: None.
 3849:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3850:	** Updated:          19-Feb-2013 by csakthiv
 3851:	**==========================================================================*/
 3852:	UINT8 hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_timeout_value(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8)
 3853:	{
 3854:	  UINT8             fl_timeOut_U8 = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_id_U8].timeout;
 3855:	  #ifdef HMI_MENU_TIMEOUT_CONGIF_FEATURE_ENABLE
 3856:	  if(HMI_MENU_ITEM_TIMEOUT_CONFIGURABLE == fl_timeOut_U8)
 3857:	  {
 3858:	      fl_timeOut_U8 = HMI_MENU_TIMEOUT_INTERFACE();
 3859:	  }
 3860:	    #endif
 3861:	  return(fl_timeOut_U8);
 3862:	}
 3863:	
 3864:	/*============================================================================
 3865:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Set_ParentFocus
 3866:	** Visibility:       global
 3867:	** Description:      This function sets the fouces marker while coming back
 3868:	**                   and loading the Parent Menu from SubMenu.
 3869:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi logic
 3870:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_cntrl_ptr, p_sub_menu_ptr
 3871:	** Critical Section: None.
 3872:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3873:	** Updated:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 3874:	**==========================================================================*/
 3875:	static void hmi_menu_logic_Set_ParentFocus(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_ptr, const MENU_DEF_T *p_sub_menu_ptr)
 3876:	{
 3877:	    UINT16       ind;
 3878:	    UINT8          fl_get_itm_entry_type_U8;
 3879:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3880:	    UINT8          fl_get_itm_absl_indx_U8;
 3881:	#endif
 3882:	    MENU_DEF_T const   * fl_get_sub_menu_SP;
 3883:	    MENU_DEF_T const   * fl_menu_def_SP;
 3884:	    UINT8 fl_menu_level;
 3885:	
 3886:	    if (p_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL)
 3887:	    {
 3888:	        fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id];
 3889:	        for (ind = 0; ind <p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr; ind++)
 3890:	        {
 3891:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3892:	            fl_get_itm_absl_indx_U8  = p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[ind];
