 3893:	            CLEARBIT(fl_get_itm_absl_indx_U8, BIT7);
 3894:	            fl_get_itm_entry_type_U8 = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_get_itm_absl_indx_U8].item_entry_type;
 3895:	            fl_get_sub_menu_SP       = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_get_itm_absl_indx_U8].utype.sub_menu;
 3896:	            if ((fl_get_itm_entry_type_U8 == MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_MENU) && (fl_get_sub_menu_SP == p_sub_menu_ptr))
 3897:	            {
 3898:	#else
 3899:	            fl_get_itm_entry_type_U8  = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[ind].item_entry_type;
 3900:	            fl_get_sub_menu_SP        = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[ind].utype.sub_menu;
 3901:	            if ((fl_get_itm_entry_type_U8 == MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_MENU) && (fl_get_sub_menu_SP == p_sub_menu_ptr))
 3902:	            {
 3903:	#endif
 3904:	                /* Set parent focus to the current menu */
 3905:	                p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16 = ind;
 3906:	                break;
 3907:	            }
 3908:	        }
 3909:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_HISTORY_HANDLING_FEATURE
 3910:	        /* Below code is to store the focus entry when the incoming menu(p_sub_menu_ptr) is hidden dynamically.
 3911:	        In this case above for loop will be failed to update the history as fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array does
 3912:	        not containg the incoming menu item. So by default the focus entry will be pointing to zero. This value
 3913:	        needs to updated in the history*/
 3914:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 3915:	        HMI_MENU_HISTORY_STORE_MENU_ITEM(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id, hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(p_menu_cntrl_ptr));
 3916:	#else
 3917:	        HMI_MENU_HISTORY_STORE_MENU_ITEM(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id, p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16);
 3918:	#endif
 3919:	#endif
 3920:	        fl_menu_level = hmi_menu_logic_get_Menu_level(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 3921:	        if(fl_menu_level > 2)
 3922:	        {
 3923:	            p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_level_U8 = 2;
 3924:	        }
 3925:	        else
 3926:	        {
 3927:	            p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_level_U8 = fl_menu_level;      
 3928:	        }
 3929:	    }
 3930:	}
 3931:	/*============================================================================
 3932:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Is_CurrentMenuOpen
 3933:	** Visibility:       global
 3934:	** Description:      This function Checks whether the focused Menu is open or not.
 3935:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 3936:	** Inputs:           None
 3937:	** Outputs:          BOOLEAN
 3938:	** Critical Section: None.
 3939:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep

 4156:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_load_menu_level
 4157:	** Visibility:       global
 4158:	** Description:      This function shall be used to load the menu level element
 4159:	**                   in the Menu control structure.

 4165:	**==========================================================================*/
 4166:	static void hmi_menu_logic_load_menu_level(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_ptr)
 4167:	{
 4168:	  if(p_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL)
 4169:	  {
 4170:	    if(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].parent_menu != FNULL)
 4171:	    {
 4172:	      /* Find the level */
 4173:	      if(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].parent_menu->parent_menu != FNULL)
 4174:	      {
 4175:	        p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_level_U8 = HMI_MENU_LEVEL_2;
 4176:	      }
 4177:	      else
 4178:	      {
 4179:	        p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_level_U8 = HMI_MENU_LEVEL_1;
 4180:	      }
 4181:	    }
 4182:	    else
 4183:	    {
 4184:	      /* It should be ROOT Menu */
 4185:	      p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_level_U8 = HMI_MENU_LEVEL_0;
 4186:	    }
 4187:	  }
 4188:	}

 4311:	                fl_ret_CB_value_e = p_CB_result_e;

 4314:	            {

 4324:	                 * ANIM_MAX_ID will be animation id e.g., Right wiper animation and then immediate down press*/
 4325:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_id_U8, fl_menu_screen_id_U8
 4326:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4327:	                    , ANIM_MAX_ID
 4328:	#endif
 4329:	                    , fl_menu_screen_action_U8);
 4330:	#endif
 4331:	                fl_ret_CB_value_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_ANIMATION;
 4332:	            }
 4333:	            else if((p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_LEFT_WAIT_ANM)||
 4334:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_RIGHT_WAIT_ANM)||
 4335:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_DOUBLE_WIPER_WAIT_ANM))
 4336:	            {
 4337:	                if((hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_id_U8].properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 4338:	                {
 4339:	                    /* Start the wait temp timer */
 4340:	                    hmi_menu_logic_start_timer(MENU_CORE_WAIT_TIMER,fl_menu_id_U8);
 4341:	                }
 4342:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_id_U8, fl_menu_screen_id_U8
 4343:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4344:	                    , fl_anim_id_U8
 4345:	#endif
 4346:	                    , fl_menu_screen_action_U8);
 4347:	                fl_ret_CB_value_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT;
 4348:	            }
 4349:	            else if((p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_FOCUS_MOVE_UP_ANM)||
 4350:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_FOCUS_MOVE_DOWN_ANM)||
 4351:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WNDW_MOVE_UP_ANM)||
 4352:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WNDW_MOVE_DOWN_ANM)||
 4353:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_LEFT_ANM)||
 4354:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_RIGHT_ANM)||
 4355:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_DOUBLE_WIPER_ANM)||
 4356:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_REBUILD_ANM)||
 4357:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_SCALE_ADJ_LEFT_ANM)||
 4358:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_SCALE_ADJ_RIGHT_ANM))
 4359:	            {
 4360:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_id_U8, fl_menu_screen_id_U8
 4361:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4362:	                    , fl_anim_id_U8
 4363:	#endif
 4364:	                    , fl_menu_screen_action_U8);
 4365:	                fl_ret_CB_value_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_ANIMATION;
 4366:	            }
 4367:	            else if((p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WRAP_RND_UP_ANM)||
 4368:	                (p_CB_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WRAP_RND_DOWN_ANM))
 4369:	            {
 4370:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_id_U8, fl_menu_screen_id_U8
 4371:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4372:	                    , fl_anim_id_U8
 4373:	#endif
 4374:	                    , fl_menu_screen_action_U8);
 4375:	                fl_ret_CB_value_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 4376:	            }
 4377:	            else
 4378:	            {
 4379:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_id_U8, fl_menu_screen_id_U8
 4380:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4381:	                    , ANIM_MAX_ID
 4382:	#endif
 4383:	                    , fl_menu_screen_action_U8);
 4384:	                fl_ret_CB_value_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 4385:	            }
 4386:	        }
 4387:	    }
 4388:	    return(fl_ret_CB_value_e);
 4389:	}
 4390:	#endif
 4391:	
 4392:	/*============================================================================
 4393:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_load_absl_index
 4394:	** Visibility:       global
 4395:	** Description:      This function shall load the ansolute index value of the
 4396:	**                   menu items.
 4397:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 4398:	** Inputs:           MENU_CONTROL_T *ptr
 4399:	** Outputs:          None.
 4400:	** Critical Section: None.
 4401:	** Created:          25-Aug-2010 by jpradeep
 4402:	** Updated:          25-Aug-2010 by jpradeep
 4403:	**==========================================================================*/
 4404:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 4405:	static void hmi_menu_logic_load_absl_index(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_ptr)
 4406:	{
 4407:	  /*Any change in this function will also affect Menu_Reload interface*/
 4408:	  UINT8 fl_get_ret_val_U8;
 4409:	  UINT16 fl_get_item_pro_U16;
 4410:	  UINT8 fl_new_index_U8;
 4411:	  UINT8 fl_old_index_U8;
 4412:	  UINT8 i;
 4413:	  #ifdef HMI_MENU_FLOATING_MENU_FEATURE
 4414:	  UINT8 fl_floating_menu_focus_entry_U8;
 4415:	  #endif
 4416:	  BOOLEAN fl_itm_enable_cb_st_BOOL;
 4417:	  MENU_DEF_T  const    *fl_menu_def_SP;
 4418:	
 4419:	  fl_itm_enable_cb_st_BOOL = FALSE;
 4420:	  if(p_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL)
 4421:	  {
 4422:	    fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id];
 4423:	    /* check the presence of menu item enable cb function */
 4424:	    if(fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL)
 4425:	    {
 4426:	      fl_itm_enable_cb_st_BOOL = TRUE;
 4427:	    }
 4428:	    
 4429:	      hmi_menu_logic_intialize_load_absl_index(p_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 4430:	
 4431:	      for (fl_old_index_U8 = 0; fl_old_index_U8 < p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr; fl_old_index_U8++)
 4432:	    {
 4433:	      /* check for the configuration function */
 4434:	      if(FALSE != fl_itm_enable_cb_st_BOOL)
 4435:	      {
 4436:	        fl_get_item_pro_U16 = fl_menu_def_SP->entry_array[fl_old_index_U8].item_properties;
 4437:	        fl_new_index_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_next_free_absl_index(p_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 4438:	        if((fl_get_item_pro_U16 & MENU_ITEM_PRO_CONFIGURABLE) != 0)
 4439:	        {
 4440:	          #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_WITH_APP_INDEX_FEATURE
 4441:	             fl_get_ret_val_U8 = fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb(fl_old_index_U8,p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 4442:	              #else
 4443:	             fl_get_ret_val_U8 = fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb(fl_old_index_U8);
 4444:	              #endif
 4445:	          if((MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_ACTIVE == fl_get_ret_val_U8)||(MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_GRAY_OUT == fl_get_ret_val_U8))
 4446:	          {
 4447:	            p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_new_index_U8] = fl_old_index_U8;
 4448:	            if(MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_GRAY_OUT == fl_get_ret_val_U8)
 4449:	            {
 4450:	              SETBIT(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_new_index_U8], BIT7);
 4451:	            }
 4452:	          }
 4453:	              #ifdef HMI_MENU_FLOATING_MENU_FEATURE
 4454:	              else if(MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FLOATING == fl_get_ret_val_U8)
 4455:	              {
 4456:	                   fl_floating_menu_focus_entry_U8 = HMI_MENU_LOGIC_GET_FLOATING_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id,fl_old_index_U8);
 4457:	                   p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_floating_menu_focus_entry_U8] = fl_old_index_U8;
 4458:	          }
 4459:	              #endif
 4460:	              else if(MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_INACTIVE == fl_get_ret_val_U8)
 4461:	              {
 4462:	            p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_new_index_U8] = 0xFE; /*index inactive replace it with active index*/
 4463:	          }
 4464:	              else
 4465:	              {
 4466:	                 /* do nothing */
 4467:	              }
 4468:	        }
 4469:	        else
 4470:	        {
 4471:	          /* Load item as default */
 4472:	          p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_new_index_U8] = fl_old_index_U8;
 4473:	        }
 4474:	      }
 4475:	      else
 4476:	      {
 4477:	        /* Load all items as default */
 4478:	        p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_old_index_U8] = fl_old_index_U8;
 4479:	      }
 4480:	    }
 4481:	    p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr = hmi_menu_logic_arrange_absl_index(p_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 4482:	      if(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr == 0)
 4483:	      {
 4484:	        /* if none of the item is active, initalize item absl to 0 focus entry so that always first item's
 4485:	        screen will be displayed instead of accesssing invalid focus entry location. Previsouly in get_screen_id 
 4486:	        it was accessing FE or FF focus entry's screen id */
 4487:	        for(i = 0; i < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS; i++)
 4488:	      {
 4489:	        p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[i] = 0;
 4490:	      }
 4491:	    }
 4492:	  }
 4493:	}
 4494:	
 4495:	/*============================================================================
 4496:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_intialize_load_absl_index_array
 4497:	** Visibility:       global
 4498:	** Description:      This function intialize load absolute index array to 0xFF
 4499:	**                   reference.
 4500:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 4501:	** Inputs:           p_menu_cntrl_ptr
 4502:	** Outputs:          
 4503:	** Critical Section: None.
 4504:	** Created:          23-Jan-2013 csakthiv
 4505:	** Updated:          
 4506:	**==========================================================================*/
 4507:	static void hmi_menu_logic_intialize_load_absl_index(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_ptr)
 4508:	{
 4509:	  UINT8 i;
 4510:	  for(i = 0; i < p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr; i++)
 4511:	  {
 4512:	    p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[i] = 0xFF;
 4513:	  }
 4514:	}
 4515:	/*============================================================================
 4516:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_next_free_absl_index
 4517:	** Visibility:       global
 4518:	** Description:      This function gives next available free index 
 4519:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 4520:	** Inputs:           MENU_CONTROL_T *ptr, fl_current_index_U8
 4521:	** Outputs:          next free index
 4522:	** Critical Section: None.
 4523:	** Created:          23-Jan-2013 csakthiv
 4524:	** Updated:          
 4525:	**==========================================================================*/
 4526:	static UINT8 hmi_menu_logic_get_next_free_absl_index(MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_ptr)
 4527:	{
 4528:	  UINT8 fl_current_index_U8;
 4529:	
 4530:	  for(fl_current_index_U8 = 0; fl_current_index_U8 < p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr; fl_current_index_U8++)
 4531:	  {
 4532:	    if(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_current_index_U8] == 0xFF)
 4533:	      {
 4534:	        break;
 4535:	      }
 4536:	  }
 4537:	  return fl_current_index_U8;
 4538:	}
 4539:	/*============================================================================
 4540:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_arrange_absl_index
 4541:	** Visibility:       global
 4542:	** Description:      This function arranges absl index i.e., removing invalid/blank index 
 4543:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 4544:	** Inputs:           MENU_CONTROL_T *ptr, fl_current_index_U8
 4545:	** Outputs:          next free index
 4546:	** Critical Section: None.
 4547:	** Created:          23-Jan-2013 csakthiv
 4548:	** Updated:          
 4549:	**==========================================================================*/
 4550:	static UINT8 hmi_menu_logic_arrange_absl_index(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_ptr)
 4551:	{
 4552:	  UINT8 fl_old_index_U8;
 4553:	  UINT8 fl_new_index_U8;
 4554:	  fl_new_index_U8 = 0;
 4555:	
 4556:	    for (fl_old_index_U8 = 0; fl_old_index_U8 < p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr; fl_old_index_U8++)
 4557:	  {
 4558:	    /* both free and inactive are considered as free index locations*/
 4559:	    if((p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_old_index_U8]!= 0xFF)&&
 4560:	      (p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_old_index_U8]!= 0xFE))
 4561:	      {
 4562:	      p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_new_index_U8] = p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_old_index_U8];
 4563:	        fl_new_index_U8++;
 4564:	      }
 4565:	  }
 4566:	  /* returns total number of entries in the menu */
 4567:	  return fl_new_index_U8;
 4568:	}
 4569:	UINT16 hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_ptr)
 4570:	{
 4571:	     UINT16 fl_focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 4572:	     if((p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) != 0)
 4573:	     {
 4574:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16 < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS)
 4575:	        {
 4576:	           fl_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16];
 4577:	        }
 4578:	        else
 4579:	        {
 4580:	           fl_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 4581:	        }
 4582:	     }  
 4583:	     else
 4584:	     {
 4585:	        if(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16 < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS)
 4586:	        {
 4587:	           fl_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16];
 4588:	        }
 4589:	     } 
 4590:	     return fl_focus_entry_U16;   
 4591:	}
 4592:	#endif
 4593:	/*============================================================================
 4594:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Get_item_entry_array
 4595:	** Visibility:       global
 4596:	** Description:      This function shall give the menu item entry array
 4597:	**                   reference.
 4598:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 4599:	** Inputs:           MENU_CONTROL_T *ptr, p_focus_entry_U16
 4600:	** Outputs:          MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_T
 4601:	** Critical Section: None.
 4602:	** Created:          25-Aug-2010 by jpradeep
 4603:	** Updated:          25-Aug-2010 by jpradeep
 4604:	**==========================================================================*/
 4605:	MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_T   *hmi_menu_logic_Get_item_entry_array(MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_ptr, UINT16 p_focus_entry_U16)
 4606:	{
 4607:	  UINT8 fl_get_absl_index_U8;
 4608:	  UINT16 fl_total_entry_nbr_U16;
 4609:	  MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_T    *fl_menu_item_entry_sp;
 4610:	
 4611:	  fl_menu_item_entry_sp = FNULL;
 4612:	  if(p_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL)
 4613:	  {
 4614:	
 4615:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 4616:	    fl_get_absl_index_U8  = p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[p_focus_entry_U16];
 4617:	    /* clear Gray out item bit */
 4618:	    CLEARBIT(fl_get_absl_index_U8, BIT7);
 4619:	#else
 4620:	    fl_get_absl_index_U8  = p_focus_entry_U16;
 4621:	#endif
 4622:	    /* so if it is dynamic with focus entry greater than entry_nbr then , null will be returned */
 4623:	    fl_total_entry_nbr_U16 = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].total_entry_nbr;
 4624:	    if(p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16 < fl_total_entry_nbr_U16)
 4625:	    {
 4626:	       fl_menu_item_entry_sp = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].entry_array[fl_get_absl_index_U8];
 4627:	    }
 4628:	    else
 4629:	    {
 4630:	       /* if dynamic menu, send the item entry pointer of zeroth entry, as focus entry is greater than menu entry nbr*/
 4631:	       fl_get_absl_index_U8 = 0;
 4632:	       fl_menu_item_entry_sp = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].entry_array[fl_get_absl_index_U8];
 4633:	    }  
 4634:	  }
 4635:	  return(fl_menu_item_entry_sp);
 4636:	}
 4637:	
 4638:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 4639:	/*============================================================================
 4640:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_is_all_menu_items_configured
 4641:	** Visibility:       global
 4642:	** Description:      This function shall be used to check the status about
 4643:	**                   the configuration of all items in the Menu.
 4644:	**                   This function shall be used if all the Menu items are
 4645:	**                   NVM configured.
 4646:	**                   It doesn't suit for the case if any one of the item is
 4647:	**                   displayed only when all its submenu items are configured.
 4648:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 4649:	** Inputs:           MENU_LIST_ID_T
 4650:	** Outputs:          TRUE/FALSE
 4651:	** Critical Section: None.
 4652:	** Created:          25-Aug-2010 by jpradeep
 4653:	** Updated:          24-Jul-2013 by csakthiv
 4654:	**==========================================================================*/
 4655:	UINT8 hmi_menu_logic_is_any_menu_item_configured(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_ID_e)
 4656:	{
 4657:	  UINT8 i;
 4658:	  UINT8 fl_ret_st_U8;
 4659:	  MENU_DEF_T  const    *fl_menu_def_SP;
 4660:	
 4661:	  /* if cb is null then return value will be active*/
 4662:	  fl_ret_st_U8 = MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_ACTIVE;
 4663:	  fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_ID_e];
 4664:	
 4665:	  if(fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL)
 4666:	  {
 4667:	    for(i=0; i<fl_menu_def_SP->total_entry_nbr; i++)
 4668:	    {
 4669:	      #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_WITH_APP_INDEX_FEATURE
 4670:	      fl_ret_st_U8 = fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb(i,p_menu_ID_e);
 4671:	      #else
 4672:	      fl_ret_st_U8 = fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb(i);
 4673:	      #endif
 4674:	      if(MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_INACTIVE != fl_ret_st_U8)
 4675:	      {
 4676:	        break;
 4677:	      }
 4678:	    }
 4679:	  }
 4680:	  return(fl_ret_st_U8);
 4681:	}
 4682:	/****************************************************************************
 4683:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_app_get_focus_entry_val
 4684:	Description       : It gives the current focus_entry_value
 4685:	Invocation        : hmi_menu_logic_interface.c
 4686:	Parameters        : MENU_CONTROL_T *, UINT16
 4687:	Return Value      : UINT16
 4688:	Critical Section  : None
 4689:	******************************************************************************/
 4690:	UINT16 hmi_menu_logic_get_focus_entry(MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_hmi_app_ptr, UINT16 p_itm_absl_indx_U16)
 4691:	{
 4692:	  UINT8 i;
 4693:	  UINT16 fl_ret_focus_entry_val_U16;
 4694:	  UINT16 fl_absl_focus_entry_U16;
 4695:	
 4696:	  fl_ret_focus_entry_val_U16 = 0;
 4697:	  /* clear Gray out item bit */
 4698:	  CLEARBIT(p_itm_absl_indx_U16, BIT7);
 4699:	
 4700:	  for(i=0; i<p_hmi_app_ptr->menu_entry_nbr; i++)
 4701:	  {
 4702:	    fl_absl_focus_entry_U16 = p_hmi_app_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[i];
 4703:	    /* clear Gray out item bit */
 4704:	    CLEARBIT(fl_absl_focus_entry_U16, BIT7);
 4705:	    if(fl_absl_focus_entry_U16 == p_itm_absl_indx_U16)
 4706:	    {
 4707:	      fl_ret_focus_entry_val_U16 = i;
 4708:	      break;
 4709:	    }
 4710:	  }
 4711:	  return(fl_ret_focus_entry_val_U16);
 4712:	}
 4713:	/****************************************************************************
 4714:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_app_is_stored_item_enabled
 4715:	Description       : This function shall provide the stored menu item status
 4716:	                    after checking the loaded items from absl index.
 4717:	Invocation        : By hmi_menu_app_get_stored_menu_item
 4718:	Parameters        : p_menu_cntrl_ptr, p_stored_item_U8
 4719:	Return Value      : BOOLEAN
 4720:	Critical Section  : None
 4721:	******************************************************************************/
 4722:	BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_is_stored_item_enabled(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_ID_e, UINT8 p_stored_item_U8)
 4723:	{
 4724:	  UINT8 i;
 4725:	  UINT8 fl_get_absl_indx_val_U8;
 4726:	  BOOLEAN fl_item_config_BOOL;
 4727:	  MENU_CONTROL_T *fl_menu_inst_ptr;
 4728:	
 4729:	  fl_item_config_BOOL = FALSE;
 4730:	  fl_menu_inst_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_menu_ID_e);
 4731:	
 4732:	  CLEARBIT(p_stored_item_U8, BIT7); /*clear gray out bit*/
 4733:	
 4734:	  if(fl_menu_inst_ptr != FNULL)
 4735:	  {
 4736:	    for(i=0; i<fl_menu_inst_ptr->menu_entry_nbr; i++)
 4737:	    {
 4738:	      fl_get_absl_indx_val_U8 = fl_menu_inst_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[i];
 4739:	      CLEARBIT(fl_get_absl_indx_val_U8, BIT7); /*clear gray out bit*/
 4740:	      if(fl_get_absl_indx_val_U8 == p_stored_item_U8)
 4741:	      {
 4742:	        fl_item_config_BOOL = TRUE;
 4743:	        break;
 4744:	      }
 4745:	    }
 4746:	  }
 4747:	  return(fl_item_config_BOOL);
 4748:	}
 4749:	#endif /* End of HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE */
 4750:	
 4751:	/*============================================================================
 4752:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Update
 4753:	** Visibility:       global   
 4754:	** Description:      This function triggers the reloading of Menu without 
 4755:	                     changing focus and finally triggers the presentation.
 4756:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application
 4757:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_id_U8
 4758:	** Critical Section: None.
 4759:	** Created:          29-Mar-2010 by jpradeep
 4760:	** Updated:          15-Oct-2013 by csakthiv
 4761:	**==========================================================================*/
 4762:	void hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Update(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_id_U8)
 4763:	{
 4764:	    MENU_CONTROL_T        *fl_menu_def_inst_ptr;
 4765:	    UINT8                  fl_get_menu_inst_U8;
 4766:	    UINT16                 fl_store_menu_entry_nbr_U16;
 4767:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T       fl_cb_result_e;
 4768:	    MENU_DEF_T  const     *fl_menu_def_SP;
 4769:	    MENU_ANIM_DATA_T       fl_menu_anim_data;
 4770:	    #if(HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_REBUILD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_INCR_TILL_VALID) || (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_REBUILD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_DECR_TILL_VALID)
 4771:	    UINT16                 fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16;
 4772:	    #endif
 4773:	    fl_cb_result_e        = MENU_CB_ACTION_COMPLETED;
 4774:	    fl_menu_def_inst_ptr  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_menu_id_U8);
 4775:	    fl_get_menu_inst_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_menu_id_U8);
 4776:	
 4777:	    if((fl_menu_def_inst_ptr != NULL)&&(fl_get_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 4778:	    {
 4779:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16  = fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 4780:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E       = fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_id;
 4781:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 4782:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8    = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_def_inst_ptr);
 4783:	        fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_id];
 4784:	        if(hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_id].cb != NULL)
 4785:	        {
 4786:	            fl_store_menu_entry_nbr_U16 = fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_entry_nbr;
 4787:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 4788:	                /* check the presence of menu item enable cb function */
 4789:	                if(fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL)
 4790:	                {
 4791:	                    fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_entry_nbr =  hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_id].total_entry_nbr;
 4792:	                    hmi_menu_logic_load_absl_index(fl_menu_def_inst_ptr);
 4793:	                }
 4794:	            #else
 4795:	                /*if HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE is not enabled,then application will do config feature check and update the entry nbr in
 4796:	                MENU_ACTION_REBUILD callback*/
 4797:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_id].cb(MENU_ACTION_REBUILD, fl_menu_def_inst_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 4798:	            #endif
 4799:	            if(fl_store_menu_entry_nbr_U16 != fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_entry_nbr)
 4800:	            {
 4801:	                fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->focus_entry_U16    = 0;
 4802:	                fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->focus_line_U16     = 0;
 4803:	                fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->selected_entry_U16 = MENU_INVALID_ENTRY;
 4804:	            }
 4805:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 4806:	                /* for dynamic menu application will update selection marker in MENU_ACTION_REBUILD callback */
 4807:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_id].cb(MENU_ACTION_REBUILD, fl_menu_def_inst_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 4808:	            #endif
 4809:	        }
 4810:	        #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4811:	            if(fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)
 4812:	            {
 4813:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_REBUILD_ANM;
 4814:	            }
 4815:	            else
 4816:	            {
 4817:	                /* menu chart will enter into wait state*/
 4818:	            }
 4819:	        #endif
 4820:	
 4821:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 4822:	            /*Here we are checking the 'is_menu_item_enable_cb != null' validation to ensure that absolute
 4823:	            index is updated for dynamic menu.*/
 4824:	            /*if static || (dynamic && is_menu_item_enable_cb != null && focus_entry < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS) */
 4825:	            if(((fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) == 0) ||
 4826:	                ((fl_menu_def_SP->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL) && (fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->focus_entry_U16 < HMI_MAX_MENU_ITEMS)))
 4827:	            {
 4828:	#if (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_REBUILD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_INCR_TILL_VALID)
 4829:	                fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16 =  hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(fl_menu_def_inst_ptr);
 4830:	                if(TESTBIT(fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16,BIT7))
 4831:	                {
 4832:	                    /* on update if the current focus entry is greyout, then increment fe till valid fe */
 4833:	                    hmi_menu_logic_down_focus_entry(fl_menu_def_inst_ptr);
 4834:	                }
 4835:	#elif (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_REBUILD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_DECR_TILL_VALID)
 4836:	                fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->focus_entry_U16];
 4837:	                if(TESTBIT(fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16,BIT7))
 4838:	                {
 4839:	                    /* on update if the current focus entry is greyout, then decrement fe till valid fe */
 4840:	                    hmi_menu_logic_up_focus_entry(fl_menu_def_inst_ptr);
 4841:	                }
 4842:	#else
 4843:	                /* do nothing.keep the focus entry in the greyed out item itself */
 4844:	#endif
 4845:	            }
 4846:	#endif
 4847:	        /*when rebuild happens need to expand if needed*/
 4848:	        #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4849:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 4850:	                MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 4851:	            #endif
 4852:	        #endif
 4853:	
 4854:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 4855:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E     = fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_id;
 4856:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 4857:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_def_inst_ptr);
 4858:	
 4859:	        #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4860:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 4861:	                fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 4862:	                (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 4863:	                fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 4864:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 4865:	            #else
 4866:	                fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 4867:	                fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 4868:	            #endif
 4869:	        #else
 4870:	            hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_def_inst_ptr->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 4871:	            #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 4872:	                , ANIM_MAX_ID
 4873:	            #endif
 4874:	                , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 4875:	        #endif
 4876:	        /*Update the callback result to menu core chart like animation,please wait or completed*/
 4877:	        hmi_menu_chart_menu_rebuild(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_get_menu_inst_U8],fl_cb_result_e);
 4878:	    }
 4879:	}
 4880:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 4881:	/****************************************************************************
 4882:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_logic_Menu_reload
 4883:	Description       : This function is used to load the Menu freshly whenever
 4884:	                    Menu item is deconfigured or conditions becomes invalid
 4885:	                    inorder to show the display either Gray out or completely
 4886:	                    removal.This function is called priodically from application.
 4887:	Invocation        : By hmi application file
 4888:	Parameters        : fl_menu_id_U8
 4889:	Return Value      : None
 4890:	Critical Section  : None
 4891:	******************************************************************************/
 4892:	void hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Reload(MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_menu_id_U8)
 4893:	{
 4894:	  MENU_DEF_T  const   *fl_current_menu_ref_ptr;
 4895:	  MENU_DEF_T  const   *fl_parent_menu_def_ptr;
 4896:	  MENU_DEF_T  const   *fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr;
 4897:	  MENU_DEF_T  const   *fl_menu_def_SP;
 4898:	  MENU_CONTROL_T      *fl_menu_cntrl_ptr;
 4899:	  MENU_CONTROL_T       fl_menu_cntrl_copy;
 4900:	  MENU_CB_RESULT_T     fl_cb_result_e;
 4901:	  MENU_LIST_ID_T       fl_get_menu_ID_index_e;
 4902:	  MENU_ANIM_DATA_T     fl_menu_anim_data;
 4903:	  BOOLEAN              fl_disp_reload_BOOL;
 4904:	  BOOLEAN              fl_consider_prnt_menu_ptr_BOOL;
 4905:	  UINT8                fl_menu_level_cnt_U8;
 4906:	  UINT8                fl_dst_menu_level_cnt_U8;
 4907:	  UINT8                fl_menu_inst_no_U8;
 4908:	  UINT8                fl_get_itm_status_U8;
 4909:	  UINT8                fl_get_item_indx_U8;
 4910:	  UINT8                fl_level_cnt_difference_U8;
 4911:	  UINT8                i;
 4912:	  #if (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_RELOAD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_TO_ZER0)
 4913:	  UINT16               fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16;
 4914:	  #endif
 4915:	  #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_WITH_APP_INDEX_FEATURE
 4916:	  MENU_LIST_ID_T       fl_item_cb_menu_id_e;
 4917:	  #endif
 4918:	  fl_disp_reload_BOOL            = FALSE;
 4919:	  fl_consider_prnt_menu_ptr_BOOL = FALSE;
 4920:	  fl_menu_cntrl_ptr              = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(fl_menu_id_U8);
 4921:	  fl_menu_inst_no_U8             = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_menu_id_U8);
 4922:	  fl_get_itm_status_U8           = MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_ACTIVE;
 4923:	
 4924:	  if((fl_menu_cntrl_ptr != NULL)&&(fl_menu_inst_no_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 4925:	  {
 4926:	    fl_menu_def_SP                 = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id];
 4927:	    fl_current_menu_ref_ptr        = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id];
 4928:	    fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr        = fl_current_menu_ref_ptr;
 4929:	    fl_parent_menu_def_ptr         = fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr;
 4930:	    /* Find the Menu level */
 4931:	    fl_menu_level_cnt_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_Menu_level(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 4932:	    if(fl_menu_level_cnt_U8 != 0)
 4933:	    {
 4934:	      while(fl_disp_reload_BOOL == FALSE)
 4935:	      {
 4936:	        /* Initialize w.r.t current Menu before starting */
 4937:	        fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr = fl_current_menu_ref_ptr;
 4938:	        fl_parent_menu_def_ptr  = fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr;
 4939:	        /* parent and submenu ptr are found */
 4940:	        for(i = 0; i < fl_menu_level_cnt_U8; i++)
 4941:	        {
 4942:	          fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr = fl_parent_menu_def_ptr;
 4943:	          fl_parent_menu_def_ptr  = fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr->parent_menu;
 4944:	        }
 4945:	        /* check for the menu level count- if the item is not found on
 4946:	           any of the level other that 0 then it should be a self Menu rebuild
 4947:	           and hence the item entry array refernce is not needed */
 4948:	        if(fl_menu_level_cnt_U8 != 0)
 4949:	        {
 4950:	          /* Validate the repective item configuration */
 4951:	          fl_get_item_indx_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_item_indx_frm_prnt_submenu(fl_parent_menu_def_ptr, fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr);
 4952:	                    if ((fl_parent_menu_def_ptr->entry_array[fl_get_item_indx_U8].item_properties & MENU_ITEM_PRO_CONFIGURABLE) != 0)
 4953:	          {
 4954:	            if(fl_parent_menu_def_ptr->is_menu_item_enable_cb != FNULL)
 4955:	            {
 4956:	                 #ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_WITH_APP_INDEX_FEATURE
 4957:	                     fl_item_cb_menu_id_e = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_index(fl_parent_menu_def_ptr);
 4958:	                     fl_get_itm_status_U8 = fl_parent_menu_def_ptr->is_menu_item_enable_cb(fl_get_item_indx_U8,fl_item_cb_menu_id_e);
 4959:	                   #else
 4960:	                     fl_get_itm_status_U8 = fl_parent_menu_def_ptr->is_menu_item_enable_cb(fl_get_item_indx_U8);
 4961:	                   #endif
 4962:	            }
 4963:	             if(MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_ACTIVE != fl_get_itm_status_U8)
 4964:	             {
 4965:	               fl_disp_reload_BOOL = TRUE;
 4966:	               fl_consider_prnt_menu_ptr_BOOL = TRUE;
 4967:	             }
 4968:	          }
 4969:	          fl_menu_level_cnt_U8--;
 4970:	        }
 4971:	        else
 4972:	        {
 4973:	          fl_disp_reload_BOOL = TRUE;
 4974:	        }
 4975:	      }
 4976:	    }
 4977:	
 4978:	    /* Menu structure loading and animation triggerring logic */
 4979:	    /* count value is reloaded again since it is lost above */
 4980:	    fl_menu_level_cnt_U8     = hmi_menu_logic_get_Menu_level(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 4981:	    if(fl_consider_prnt_menu_ptr_BOOL != FALSE)
 4982:	    {
 4983:	      fl_get_menu_ID_index_e   = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_index(fl_parent_menu_def_ptr);
 4984:	      fl_dst_menu_level_cnt_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_Menu_level(fl_get_menu_ID_index_e);
 4985:	    }
 4986:	    else
 4987:	    {
 4988:	      fl_dst_menu_level_cnt_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_Menu_level(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 4989:	    }
 4990:	
 4991:	    if(fl_menu_level_cnt_U8 != fl_dst_menu_level_cnt_U8)
 4992:	    {
 4993:	      /* Intimate the Focus Menu about the Reload via callback */
 4994:	      if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 4995:	        {
 4996:	          (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_RELOAD, fl_menu_cntrl_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 4997:	      }
 4998:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 4999:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E     = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id;
 5000:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 5001:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 5002:	      (void)hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(fl_parent_menu_def_ptr, fl_submenu_menu_def_ptr, fl_menu_inst_no_U8, MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_CHILD);
 5003:	      /* Reload the menu cntrl ptr to ensure that we are pointing the correct instance after
 5004:	         closing the menu instance if opened. It is really needed only when there is a instance
 5005:	         but for additional robustness it is newly fetched */
 5006:	        hmi_menu_logic_update_current_focus_menu_ptr();
 5007:	      fl_menu_cntrl_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_required_menu_instance(fl_menu_inst_no_U8);
 5008:	        #if (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_RELOAD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_TO_ZER0)
 5009:	        fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 5010:	          if(TESTBIT(fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16,BIT7))
 5011:	          {
 5012:	            /* on reload and on opening parent menu, if the focus entry is greyed ,then move first entry*/
 5013:	              fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 5014:	          }
 5015:	        #endif
 5016:	      hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 5017:	
 5018:	
 5019:	      fl_level_cnt_difference_U8 = fl_menu_level_cnt_U8 - fl_dst_menu_level_cnt_U8;
 5020:	      hmi_lsh_freeze_button_events_ext(fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8,fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E);
 5021:	
 5022:	      if(fl_level_cnt_difference_U8 > HMI_MENU_LEVEL_1)
 5023:	      {
 5024:	        fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_DOUBLE_WIPER_ANM;
 5025:	      }
 5026:	      else
 5027:	      {
 5028:	        fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_LEFT_WAIT_ANM;
 5029:	      }
 5030:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 5031:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E     = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id;
 5032:	        fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 5033:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 5034:	
 5035:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5036:	      #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 5037:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 5038:	      (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 5039:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 5040:	      (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 5041:	      #else
 5042:	      fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 5043:	      (void) hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 5044:	      #endif    
 5045:	#else
 5046:	      if(MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT == fl_cb_result_e)
 5047:	      {
 5048:	        hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 5049:	      }
 5050:	      else
 5051:	      {
 5052:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 5053:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5054:	                    , ANIM_MAX_ID
 5055:	#endif
 5056:	                    , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 5057:	      }
 5058:	#endif
 5059:	    }
 5060:	    else
 5061:	    {
 5062:	      (void)memcpy((void *)&fl_menu_cntrl_copy, (void *)fl_menu_cntrl_ptr, sizeof(MENU_CONTROL_T));
 5063:	      fl_menu_cntrl_copy.menu_entry_nbr = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_cntrl_copy.menu_id].total_entry_nbr;
 5064:	        /* new menu_entry_nbr will be updated in fl_menu_cntrl_copy */
 5065:	      hmi_menu_logic_load_absl_index(&fl_menu_cntrl_copy);
 5066:	      
 5067:	        if((fl_menu_cntrl_copy.menu_entry_nbr == 0)&&((fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_GOTO_PARENT_ON_ZERO_ENTRY) != 0))
 5068:	        {
 5069:	        if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 5070:	        {
 5071:	           (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_RELOAD, &fl_menu_cntrl_copy, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 5072:	        }
 5073:	          hmi_menu_logic_open_parent_menu(fl_menu_cntrl_copy.menu_id);
 5074:	            #if (HMI_MENU_SET_FOCUS_ENTRY_RELOAD_GREYOUT == HMI_MENU_FOCUS_ENTRY_TO_ZER0)
 5075:	          fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16 = hmi_menu_logic_get_absl_index_focus_entry(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 5076:	            if(TESTBIT(fl_item_absl_focus_entry_U16,BIT7))
 5077:	            {
 5078:	              /* on reload and on opening parent menu, if the focus entry is greyed ,then move first entry*/
 5079:	                fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 5080:	            }
 5081:	          #endif
 5082:	        }
 5083:	      else if(fl_menu_cntrl_copy.menu_entry_nbr != fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr)
 5084:	      {
 5085:	        hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Rebuild(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 5086:	        fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_properties |= MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC_UPDATE;
 5087:	      }
 5088:	      else
 5089:	      {
 5090:	        /*check individual index of menu are same*/
 5091:	        for(i = 0; i< fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_entry_nbr; i++)
 5092:	        {
 5093:	           if(fl_menu_cntrl_copy.menu_itm_absl_indx[i] != fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[i])
 5094:	          {
 5095:	             hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Rebuild(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 5096:	             fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_properties |= MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC_UPDATE;
 5097:	             break;
 5098:	          }
 5099:	        }
 5100:	      }
 5101:	       /* if both entry_nbr and indivudual index are same then no rebuild not required*/
 5102:	      }
 5103:	  }
 5104:	}
 5105:	#endif
 5106:	/****************************************************************************
 5107:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_logic_Menu_reload
 5108:	Description       : This function is used to load the Menu freshly whenever
 5109:	                    Menu item is deconfigured or conditions becomes invalid
 5110:	                    inorder to show the display either Gray out or completely
 5111:	                    removal
 5112:	Invocation        : By hmi application file
 5113:	Parameters        : p_menu_cntrl_ptr, p_stored_item_U8
 5114:	Return Value      : None
 5115:	Critical Section  : None
 5116:	******************************************************************************/
 5117:	static UINT8 hmi_menu_logic_get_item_indx_frm_prnt_submenu(const MENU_DEF_T *p_parent_menu_def_ptr, const MENU_DEF_T *p_submenu_menu_def_ptr)
 5118:	{
 5119:	  UINT8 i;
 5120:	
 5121:	  for(i=0; i<p_parent_menu_def_ptr->total_entry_nbr; i++)
 5122:	  {
 5123:	    if((p_parent_menu_def_ptr->entry_array[i].item_entry_type == MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_MENU)||
 5124:	       (p_parent_menu_def_ptr->entry_array[i].item_entry_type == MENU_ITEM_ENTRY_TYPE_EXE))
 5125:	    {
 5126:	      if(p_parent_menu_def_ptr->entry_array[i].utype.sub_menu == p_submenu_menu_def_ptr)
 5127:	      {
 5128:	        break;
 5129:	      }
 5130:	    }
 5131:	  }
 5132:	  return(i);
 5133:	}
 5134:	
 5135:	/****************************************************************************
 5136:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_logic_get_Menu_level
 5137:	Description       : This function is used to get the Requested Menu level value
 5138:	Invocation        : By hmi application file
 5139:	Parameters        : p_menu_ID_e
 5140:	Return Value      : None
 5141:	Critical Section  : None
 5142:	******************************************************************************/
 5143:	static UINT8 hmi_menu_logic_get_Menu_level(MENU_LIST_ID_T p_menu_ID_e)
 5144:	{
 5145:	  const MENU_DEF_T *fl_menu_def_ptr;
 5146:	  UINT8 fl_menu_level_val_U8;
 5147:	
 5148:	  fl_menu_def_ptr       = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_ID_e];
 5149:	  fl_menu_level_val_U8 = 0;
 5150:	  if(p_menu_ID_e < TOTAL_MENU_ID)
 5151:	  {
 5152:	    while(fl_menu_def_ptr->parent_menu != NULL)
 5153:	    {
 5154:	      fl_menu_def_ptr = fl_menu_def_ptr->parent_menu;
 5155:	      fl_menu_level_val_U8++;
 5156:	    }
 5157:	  }
 5158:	  return(fl_menu_level_val_U8);
 5159:	}
 5160:	/****************************************************************************
 5161:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_logic_start_timer
 5162:	Description       : Common Interface to start all the timers used in Menu Core
 5163:	Invocation        : hmi_menu_core_01_chart.c
 5164:	Parameters        : timer type and menu id.
 5165:	Return Value      : None
 5166:	Critical Section  : None
 5167:	******************************************************************************/
 5168:	void hmi_menu_logic_start_timer(UINT8 fl_timer_type_U8, MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_menu_id_U8)
 5169:	{
 5170:	    MENU_CONTROL_T   *fl_menu_cntrl_SP;
 5171:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_U8;
 5172:	    UINT8 fl_timer_val_U8;
 5173:	  fl_menu_inst_U8  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5174:	    fl_menu_cntrl_SP = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5175:	
 5176:	    if((fl_menu_cntrl_SP != NULL)&&(fl_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 5177:	    {
 5178:	        switch(fl_timer_type_U8)
 5179:	        {
 5180:	        case MENU_CORE_OPEN_TIMER:
 5181:	
 5182:	            fl_timer_val_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_timeout_value(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5183:	            if(fl_timer_val_U8 != 0)
 5184:	            {
 5185:	                HMI_MENU_START_TIMER(fl_menu_id_U8, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_open_timer_id,fl_timer_val_U8);
 5186:	            }
 5187:	            else
 5188:	            {
 5189:	                /* if submenu's timeout is zero, then it will be stopped below*/
 5190:	                hmi_menu_logic_stop_timer(MENU_CORE_OPEN_TIMER,fl_menu_id_U8);
 5191:	            }
 5192:	            break;
 5193:	
 5194:	        case MENU_CORE_WAIT_TIMER:
 5195:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5196:	    
 5197:	            HMI_MENU_START_WAIT_TEMP_TIMER(fl_menu_id_U8, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_wait_timer_id,HMI_MENU_WAIT_TEMP_TIMER_VALUE);
 5198:	#endif
 5199:	            break;
 5200:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_FAST_SCROLL_ENABLE
 5201:	        case MENU_CORE_FASTSCROLL_CNT_INCR_TIMER:
 5202:	            HMI_MENU_FS_START_TIMER(fl_menu_id_U8, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_fastscroll_timer_id,HMI_MENU_FS_CNT_INCR_TIMER_VALUE);
 5203:	            break;
 5204:	        case MENU_CORE_FASTSCROLL_RUN_WAIT_TIMER:
 5205:	            HMI_MENU_FS_START_TIMER(fl_menu_id_U8, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_fastscroll_timer_id,HMI_MENU_FS_RUN_WAIT_TIMER_VALUE);
 5206:	            break;
 5207:	        case MENU_CORE_FASTSCROLL_CNT_START_TIMER:
 5208:	            HMI_MENU_FS_START_TIMER(fl_menu_id_U8, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_fastscroll_timer_id,HMI_MENU_FS_CNT_START_TIMER_VALUE);
 5209:	            break;
 5210:	#endif
 5211:	        default:
 5212:	            break;
 5213:	        }
 5214:	    }
 5215:	}
 5216:	/****************************************************************************
 5217:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_logic_stop_timer
 5218:	Description       : Common Interface to stop all the timers used in Menu Core
 5219:	Invocation        : hmi_menu_core_01_chart.c
 5220:	Parameters        : timer type and menu id.
 5221:	Return Value      : None
 5222:	Critical Section  : None
 5223:	******************************************************************************/
 5224:	void hmi_menu_logic_stop_timer(UINT8 fl_timer_type_U8, MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_menu_id_U8)
 5225:	{
 5226:	    MENU_CONTROL_T   *fl_menu_cntrl_SP;
 5227:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_U8;
 5228:	
 5229:	  fl_menu_inst_U8  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5230:	    fl_menu_cntrl_SP = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5231:	
 5232:	    if((fl_menu_cntrl_SP != NULL)&&(fl_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 5233:	    {
 5234:	        switch(fl_timer_type_U8)
 5235:	        {
 5236:	        case MENU_CORE_OPEN_TIMER:
 5237:	            HMI_MENU_STOP_TIMER(fl_menu_id_U8, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_open_timer_id);
 5238:	            break;
 5239:	
 5240:	        case MENU_CORE_WAIT_TIMER:
 5241:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5242:	    
 5243:	            HMI_MENU_STOP_WAIT_TEMP_TIMER(fl_menu_id_U8, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_wait_timer_id);
 5244:	#endif
 5245:	            break;
 5246:	
 5247:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_FAST_SCROLL_ENABLE
 5248:	        case MENU_CORE_FASTSCROLL_CNT_INCR_TIMER:
 5249:	        case MENU_CORE_FASTSCROLL_RUN_WAIT_TIMER:
 5250:	        case MENU_CORE_FASTSCROLL_CNT_START_TIMER:
 5251:	            HMI_MENU_FS_STOP_TIMER(fl_menu_id_U8, hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_fastscroll_timer_id);
 5252:	            break;
 5253:	#endif
 5254:	        default:
 5255:	            break;
 5256:	        }
 5257:	    }
 5258:	}
 5259:	/****************************************************************************
 5260:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_logic_open_timer_timeout
 5261:	Description       : Menu Open Timer timeout.
 5262:	Invocation        : hmi_menu_core_01_chart.c
 5263:	Parameters        : fl_timer_id_U8.
 5264:	Return Value      : None
 5265:	Critical Section  : None
 5266:	******************************************************************************/
 5267:	void hmi_menu_logic_open_timer_timeout(UINT8 fl_timer_id_U8
 5268:	                                  #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5269:	                                    , UINT8 fl_close_anim_id_U8
 5270:	                                  #endif
 5271:	                                  )
 5272:	{
 5273:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_U8;
 5274:	    for(fl_menu_inst_U8 = 0;fl_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE;fl_menu_inst_U8++)
 5275:	    {
 5276:	       if(hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_open_timer_id == fl_timer_id_U8)
 5277:	       {
 5278:	            
 5279:	            hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_close(MENU_ACTION_CLOSE_ON_TIMEOUT,l_menu_instance_array[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_id
 5280:	                                             #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5281:	                                              ,fl_close_anim_id_U8
 5282:	                                             #endif
 5283:	                                             );
 5284:	            break;
 5285:	       }
 5286:	    }
 5287:	}
 5288:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5289:	/****************************************************************************
 5290:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_logic_wait_timer_timeout
 5291:	Description       : Wait Timer timout. Start Please wait Anim.
 5292:	Invocation        : hmi_menu_core_01_chart.c
 5293:	Parameters        : fl_timer_id_U8.
 5294:	Return Value      : None
 5295:	Critical Section  : None
 5296:	******************************************************************************/
 5297:	void hmi_menu_logic_wait_timer_timeout(UINT8 fl_timer_id_U8)
 5298:	{
 5299:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_U8;
 5300:	    for(fl_menu_inst_U8 = 0;fl_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE;fl_menu_inst_U8++)
 5301:	    {
 5302:	       if(hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_wait_timer_id == fl_timer_id_U8)
 5303:	       {
 5304:	          hmi_menu_chart_Wait_temp_timeout(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_menu_inst_U8]);
 5305:	          break;
 5306:	       }
 5307:	    }
 5308:	}
 5309:	#endif
 5310:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_FAST_SCROLL_ENABLE
 5311:	/****************************************************************************
 5312:	Function Name     : hmi_menu_logic_fast_scroll_timer_timeout
 5313:	Description       : Fast Scroll timout. Start Fastscroll Anim.
 5314:	Invocation        : hmi_menu_core_01_chart.c
 5315:	Parameters        : fl_timer_id_U8.
 5316:	Return Value      : None
 5317:	Critical Section  : None
 5318:	******************************************************************************/
 5319:	void hmi_menu_logic_fast_scroll_timer_timeout(UINT8 fl_timer_id_U8)
 5320:	{
 5321:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_U8;
 5322:	    for(fl_menu_inst_U8 = 0;fl_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE;fl_menu_inst_U8++)
 5323:	    {
 5324:	       if(hmi_menu_inst_config_table[fl_menu_inst_U8].menu_fastscroll_timer_id == fl_timer_id_U8)
 5325:	       {
 5326:	          hmi_menu_chart_Menu_FSTimeout(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_menu_inst_U8]);
 5327:	          break;
 5328:	       }
 5329:	    }
 5330:	
 5331:	}
 5332:	#endif
 5333:	
 5334:	/*============================================================================
 5335:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_left_menu_action
 5336:	** Description:      This function shall be used to perform left action.
 5337:	** Invocation:       Invoked by logic.c
 5338:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_cntrl_ptr, menu action
 5339:	** Critical Section: None.
 5340:	** Created          : 20 sep, 2013 CSAKTHIV
 5341:	** Updated          : 
 5342:	**==========================================================================*/
 5343:	static MENU_CB_RESULT_T hmi_menu_logic_left_menu_action(MENU_CONTROL_T *p_menu_cntrl_SP, UINT8 p_menu_action_U8)
 5344:	{
 5345:	   MENU_CB_RESULT_T            fl_cb_result_e;
 5346:	   MENU_CB_RESULT_T            fl_gotoParentMenu_U8;
 5347:	   const MENU_DEF_T           *fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr;
 5348:	   const MENU_DEF_T           *fl_root_menu_ref_ptr;
 5349:	   MENU_DEF_T  const          *fl_menu_def_SP;
 5350:	   UINT8                       fl_menu_inst_no_U8;
 5351:	   UINT16                      temp;
 5352:	   BOOLEAN                     temp_1;
 5353:	
 5354:	   fl_gotoParentMenu_U8  = 0;
 5355:	   fl_cb_result_e        = MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE;
 5356:	   temp_1                = FALSE;
 5357:	
 5358:	   if(p_menu_cntrl_SP != FNULL)
 5359:	   {
 5360:	      fl_menu_inst_no_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id);
 5361:	      fl_menu_def_SP = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 5362:	      if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_level_U8 != HMI_MENU_LEVEL_0)&&(fl_menu_inst_no_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 5363:	      {
 5364:	         if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_level_U8 > HMI_MENU_LEVEL_1) &&
 5365:	            (p_menu_action_U8 == MENU_ACTION_LEFT_LONG))
 5366:	         {
 5367:	            /* Open the ROOT Menu - to be implemented */
 5368:	            /* Loading the menu level to be taken care */
 5369:	            fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr  = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 5370:	            fl_root_menu_ref_ptr = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id].parent_menu;
 5371:	            while(fl_root_menu_ref_ptr != NULL)
 5372:	            {
 5373:	              if(fl_root_menu_ref_ptr->parent_menu != NULL)
 5374:	              {
 5375:	                 fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr  = fl_root_menu_ref_ptr;
 5376:	                 fl_root_menu_ref_ptr = fl_root_menu_ref_ptr->parent_menu;
 5377:	              }
 5378:	              else
 5379:	              {
 5380:	                 fl_root_menu_ref_ptr = NULL;
 5381:	              }
 5382:	            }
 5383:	            /* post MENU_ACTION_CLOSE to existing menu*/
 5384:	            if(fl_menu_def_SP->cb != FNULL)
 5385:	            {
 5386:	              (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_CLOSE, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 5387:	            }
 5388:	#ifdef    LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5389:	            (void)hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr->parent_menu, fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr, fl_menu_inst_no_U8, MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_CHILD);
 5390:	#else
 5391:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr->parent_menu, fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr, fl_menu_inst_no_U8, MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_CHILD);
 5392:	#endif
 5393:	#if 0
 5394:	            hmi_menu_logic_Set_ParentFocus(p_menu_cntrl_SP, fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr);
 5395:	#endif
 5396:	            hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 5397:	#ifdef    LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5398:	            /* Trigger DOUBLE WIPER */
 5399:	            /* belwo commented check was there in L538 menu core and not in C520.In this release */
 5400:	            /* removed, need to anlayse its requirement                                          */
 5401:	            /* if(fl_cb_result_e == MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)*/
 5402:	            /* {*/
 5403:	               fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_DOUBLE_WIPER_ANM;
 5404:	            /* }*/
 5405:	#endif
 5406:	         }
 5407:	         else
 5408:	         {
 5409:	            /* hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_close should be called here. Will be changed in future req/redesign
 5410:	               in redesign return from application will not be considered*/
 5411:	            if (fl_menu_def_SP->cb != NULL)
 5412:	            {
 5413:	               fl_gotoParentMenu_U8 = fl_menu_def_SP->cb(MENU_ACTION_CLOSE, p_menu_cntrl_SP, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 5414:	            }
 5415:	            temp = p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC;
 5416:	            if(MENU_CB_ACTION_NONE == fl_gotoParentMenu_U8)
 5417:	            {
 5418:	               temp_1 = TRUE;
 5419:	            }
 5420:	            if((0 != temp) && (FALSE != temp_1))
 5421:	            {
 5422:	               /*
 5423:	               * Redisplay current menu
 5424:	               */
 5425:	               fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(fl_menu_def_SP, fl_menu_def_SP, fl_menu_inst_no_U8 , MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_CHILD);
 5426:	            }
 5427:	            else
 5428:	            {
 5429:	              if (fl_menu_def_SP->parent_menu != FNULL)
 5430:	              {
 5431:	                 fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_id];
 5432:	                 /* We are in a submenu: close sub menu */
 5433:	                 fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(fl_menu_def_SP->parent_menu,fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr, fl_menu_inst_no_U8, MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_CHILD);
 5434:	#if 0
 5435:	                 hmi_menu_logic_Set_ParentFocus(p_menu_cntrl_SP, fl_sub_menu_ref_ptr);
 5436:	#endif
 5437:	                 hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 5438:	              }
 5439:	            }
 5440:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5441:	            /* Trigger LEFT WIPER*/
 5442:	            if(fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)
 5443:	            {
 5444:	             fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_LEFT_ANM;
 5445:	             #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 5446:	                 MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 5447:	             #endif
 5448:	            }
 5449:	            else
 5450:	            {
 5451:	             fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_LEFT_WAIT_ANM;
 5452:	            }
 5453:	#endif
 5454:	         }
 5455:	         if((p_menu_cntrl_SP->menu_properties & MENU_PRO_DYNAMIC) == 0)
 5456:	         {
 5457:	            hmi_menu_logic_load_menu_level(p_menu_cntrl_SP);
 5458:	         }
 5459:	      }
 5460:	   }
 5461:	   return (fl_cb_result_e);
 5462:	}
 5463:	/*============================================================================
 5464:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_process_available_data
 5465:	** Description:      This function intimate menu core that requested data is available
 5466:	** Invocation:       Invoked by Application(RDAP)
 5467:	** Inputs/Outputs:   p_menu_id_U8
 5468:	** Critical Section: None.
 5469:	** Created          : 20 sep, 2013 CSAKTHIV
 5470:	** Updated          : 
 5471:	**==========================================================================*/
 5472:	void hmi_menu_logic_process_available_data(MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_menu_id_U8)

 5474:	  UINT8 fl_menu_inst_no_U8;
 5475:	  fl_menu_inst_no_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5476:	  if(fl_menu_inst_no_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 5477:	  {
 5478:	     hmi_menu_chart_DataAvailable(&l_menu_core_chart_inst_array[fl_menu_inst_no_U8]);
 5479:	  }
 5480:	}

 5495:	  {

 5497:	  }
