 5499:	/*============================================================================
 5500:	** Function Name    : hmi_menu_core_disable_timer_status
 5501:	** Visibility       : Local, Interface to the Popup Core
 5502:	** Description      : Disable the timer status
 5503:	** Invocation       : Invoked by the menu Core
 5504:	** Inputs/Outputs   : disable timer status
 5505:	** Critical Section : No
 5506:	** Created          : 20 sep, 2013 CSAKTHIV
 5507:	** Updated          : 
 5508:	**==========================================================================*/
 5509:	static void hmi_menu_logic_disable_open_timer_status(MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_menu_id_U8)
 5510:	{
 5511:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_U8;
 5512:	  fl_menu_inst_U8  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5513:	    if(fl_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 5514:	  {
 5515:	        l_menu_instance_array[fl_menu_inst_U8].timer_status_BOOL =  FALSE;
 5516:	    }
 5517:	}
 5518:	/*============================================================================
 5519:	** Function Name    : hmi_menu_core_enable_timer_status
 5520:	** Visibility       : Local, Interface to the Popup Core
 5521:	** Description      : Disable the timer status
 5522:	** Invocation       : Invoked by the menu Core
 5523:	** Inputs/Outputs   : enable timer status
 5524:	** Critical Section : No
 5525:	** Created          : 20 sep, 2013 CSAKTHIV
 5526:	** Updated          : 
 5527:	**==========================================================================*/
 5528:	static void hmi_menu_logic_enable_open_timer_status(MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_menu_id_U8)
 5529:	{
 5530:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_U8;
 5531:	  fl_menu_inst_U8  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5532:	    if(fl_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 5533:	  {
 5534:	        l_menu_instance_array[fl_menu_inst_U8].timer_status_BOOL =  TRUE;
 5535:	    }
 5536:	}
 5537:	/*============================================================================
 5538:	** Function Name    : hmi_menu_logic_is_open_timer_enabled
 5539:	** Visibility       : Local, Interface to the Popup Core
 5540:	** Description      : Disable the timer status
 5541:	** Invocation       : Invoked by the menu Core
 5542:	** Inputs/Outputs   : disable timer status
 5543:	**                    
 5544:	**                    
 5545:	** Critical Section : No
 5546:	** Created          : 20 sep, 2013 CSAKTHIV
 5547:	** Updated          : 
 5548:	**==========================================================================*/
 5549:	static BOOLEAN hmi_menu_logic_is_open_timer_enabled(MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_menu_id_U8)
 5550:	{
 5551:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_U8;
 5552:	    BOOLEAN fl_timer_status_BOOL = FALSE;
 5553:	  fl_menu_inst_U8  = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_menu_id_U8);
 5554:	    if(fl_menu_inst_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE)
 5555:	    {
 5556:	        fl_timer_status_BOOL = l_menu_instance_array[fl_menu_inst_U8].timer_status_BOOL;
 5557:	    }
 5558:	    return fl_timer_status_BOOL; 
 5559:	}
 5560:	/*============================================================================
 5561:	** Function Name    : hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_close
 5562:	** Description      : Process different menu close (timeout,close by app,close by child,parent)
 5563:	** Invocation       : Invoked by the menu Core
 5564:	** Inpu1ts/Outputs  : menu_close_action,menu_id,anim_id
 5565:	** Critical Section : No
 5566:	** Created          : 19 Oct, 2013 CSAKTHIV
 5567:	** Updated          : 
 5568:	**==========================================================================*/
 5569:	static void hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_close(UINT8 fl_menu_close_action_U8, MENU_LIST_ID_T p_close_menu_id_e
 5570:	                                                   #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5571:	                                                   ,UINT8 p_menu_anim_ID_U8
 5572:	                                                   #endif
 5573:	                                                   )
 5574:	{
 5575:	    UINT8                  fl_logic_st_id_U8;
 5576:	    UINT8                  fl_menu_inst_array_idx_U8;
 5577:	    MENU_CONTROL_T        *fl_menu_close_ptr;
 5578:	    MENU_DEF_T  const     *fl_menu_def_SP;
 5579:	
 5580:	    /* Array Access Boundary check */
 5581:	    if(p_close_menu_id_e < TOTAL_MENU_ID)
 5582:	    {
 5583:	      /* Get the menu instance array index based on menu id */
 5584:	      fl_menu_inst_array_idx_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(p_close_menu_id_e);
 5585:	      /* Find the Menu instance to be closed */
 5586:	      fl_menu_close_ptr   = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(p_close_menu_id_e);
 5587:	      /* check the close condition */
 5588:	      if((fl_menu_close_ptr != FNULL) && (fl_menu_inst_array_idx_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 5589:	      {
 5590:	         fl_menu_def_SP      = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_close_ptr->menu_id];
 5591:	         fl_logic_st_id_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_close_ptr);
 5592:	
 5593:	         switch(fl_menu_close_action_U8)
 5594:	           {
 5595:	           case MENU_ACTION_FORCED_CLOSE:
 5596:	           case MENU_ACTION_CLOSE_ON_TIMEOUT:
 5597:	              if(fl_menu_def_SP->cb != FNULL)
 5598:	              {
 5599:	                (void)fl_menu_def_SP->cb((MENU_ACTION_T)fl_menu_close_action_U8, fl_menu_close_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 5600:	              }
 5601:	              /*there is no need to check the return value from application. If menu_close is called then it is required to intimate
 5602:	                the app about close action. If return value is checked, then there will be more changes in menu core whenever menu_close is called */
 5603:	
 5604:	              /* Destroy the menu instance only when the current menu id is same as p_close_menu_id_e. */
 5605:	              hmi_menu_logic_destroy_menu_instance(p_close_menu_id_e);
 5606:	              hmi_menu_logic_update_current_focus_menu_ptr();
 5607:	                        (void) LSH_REM_STATE_EXT(fl_logic_st_id_U8, p_menu_anim_ID_U8, p_close_menu_id_e);
 5608:	                break;
 5609:	           
 5610:	           case MENU_ACTION_CLOSE:
 5611:	              /* callback called in left_action. since return value from application this cannot be included
 5612:	                 It will be changed on future redesign in which return value from application will not be considered
 5613:	                 action will be taken based on menu_level */
 5614:	              break;
 5615:	
 5616:	           default:
 5617:	              break;
 5618:	         }
 5619:	        }
 5620:	    }
 5621:	}
 5622:	
 5623:	/*============================================================================
 5624:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id
 5625:	** Visibility:       global
 5626:	** Description:      This function is to get the screen based on the focus entry
 5627:	**
 5628:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 5629:	** Inputs:           MENU_CONTROL_T *ptr, p_focus_entry_U16
 5630:	** Outputs:          LSH_STATE_ID_T
 5631:	** Critical Section: None.
 5632:	** Created:          15-Jan-2014
 5633:	** Updated:
 5634:	**==========================================================================*/
 5635:	LSH_STATE_ID_T   hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(MENU_CONTROL_T const *p_menu_cntrl_ptr)
 5636:	{
 5637:	  /*Initialize with invalid screen id*/
 5638:	  LSH_STATE_ID_T        fl_screen_id_U8 = LSH_NUMBER_OF_LOGIC_STATES;
 5639:	  
 5640:	  if (p_menu_cntrl_ptr != FNULL)
 5641:	  {
 5642:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 5643:	    UINT8                 fl_get_absl_index_U8;
 5644:	    UINT16                fl_menu_total_entry_nbr_U16;
 5645:	    UINT16                fl_focus_entry_U16;
 5646:	
 5647:	    fl_focus_entry_U16 = p_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 5648:	    fl_menu_total_entry_nbr_U16 = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].total_entry_nbr;
 5649:	    if (fl_focus_entry_U16 >= fl_menu_total_entry_nbr_U16)
 5650:	    {
 5651:	      /* may be a dynamic menu like cd track list, preset list */
 5652:	      fl_focus_entry_U16 = 0;
 5653:	    }
 5654:	#ifdef HMI_MENU_ITEM_CONFIG_FEATURE
 5655:	    fl_get_absl_index_U8 = p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_itm_absl_indx[fl_focus_entry_U16];
 5656:	    /* clear Gray out item bit */
 5657:	    CLEARBIT(fl_get_absl_index_U8, BIT7);
 5658:	#else
 5659:	    fl_get_absl_index_U8 = fl_focus_entry_U16;
 5660:	#endif
 5661:	
 5662:	    fl_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].entry_array[fl_get_absl_index_U8].item_screen_id_U8;
 5663:	#else
 5664:	    fl_screen_id_U8 = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[p_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id].disp_screen_id;
 5665:	#endif
 5666:	  }
 5667:	  return(fl_screen_id_U8);
 5668:	}
 5669:	
 5670:	/*============================================================================
 5671:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no
 5672:	** Visibility:       global
 5673:	** Description:      This function is to get the instance no for the menu id . Instance no is decided from configuration in tool.
 5674:	**
 5675:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 5676:	** Inputs:           MENU_CONTROL_T *ptr, p_focus_entry_U16
 5677:	** Outputs:          LSH_STATE_ID_T
 5678:	** Critical Section: None.
 5679:	** Created:          15-April-2014 by CSAKTHIV
 5680:	** Updated:
 5681:	**==========================================================================*/
 5682:	static UINT8 hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_menu_id_U8)
 5683:	{
 5684:	    UINT8 fl_menu_inst_no = NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE;
 5685:	  if(fl_menu_id_U8 < TOTAL_MENU_ID)
 5686:	    {
 5687:	    fl_menu_inst_no =  hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_menu_id_U8].inst_no;
 5688:	    }
 5689:	    return fl_menu_inst_no;
 5690:	}
 5691:	/*============================================================================
 5692:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_open_parent_menu
 5693:	** Visibility:       global
 5694:	** Description:      This interface opens the parent menu for the given child active menu
 5695:	**
 5696:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 5697:	** Inputs:           MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_curr_menu_id_U8
 5698:	** Outputs:          void
 5699:	** Critical Section: None.
 5700:	** Created:          09-July-2014 by CSAKTHIV
 5701:	** Updated:
 5702:	**==========================================================================*/
 5703:	void hmi_menu_logic_open_parent_menu(MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_curr_menu_id_U8)
 5704:	{
 5705:	    MENU_DEF_T  const   *fl_parent_menu_def_ptr;
 5706:	    MENU_DEF_T  const   *fl_current_menu_def_ptr;
 5707:	    UINT8                fl_menu_inst_no_U8;
 5708:	    MENU_ANIM_DATA_T     fl_menu_anim_data;
 5709:	    MENU_CONTROL_T      *fl_menu_cntrl_ptr;
 5710:	    MENU_CB_RESULT_T     fl_cb_result_e;
 5711:	
 5712:	    fl_menu_cntrl_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_instance(fl_curr_menu_id_U8);
 5713:	
 5714:	    if(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr != NULL)
 5715:	    {
 5716:	        fl_menu_inst_no_U8 = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_inst_no(fl_curr_menu_id_U8);
 5717:	        fl_current_menu_def_ptr = &hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_curr_menu_id_U8];
 5718:	        fl_parent_menu_def_ptr  = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_curr_menu_id_U8].parent_menu;
 5719:	        if((fl_parent_menu_def_ptr != NULL)&&(fl_menu_inst_no_U8 < NB_OF_MENU_INSTANCE))
 5720:	        {
 5721:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 5722:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E     = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id;
 5723:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 5724:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 5725:	
 5726:	            /* hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_close should be called here. Will be changed in future req/redesign
 5727:	            in redesign return from application will not be considered*/
 5728:	            if (fl_current_menu_def_ptr->cb != NULL)
 5729:	            {
 5730:	                (void)fl_current_menu_def_ptr->cb(MENU_ACTION_CLOSE, fl_menu_cntrl_ptr, MENU_BTN_INVALID);
 5731:	            }
 5732:	
 5733:	            fl_cb_result_e = hmi_menu_logic_SubMenu_Open(fl_parent_menu_def_ptr, fl_current_menu_def_ptr, fl_menu_inst_no_U8, MENU_ACTION_OPEN_FROM_CHILD);
 5734:	            /* Reload the menu cntrl ptr to ensure that we are pointing the correct instance after
 5735:	            closing the menu instance if opened. It is really needed only when there is a instance
 5736:	            but for additional robustness it is newly fetched */
 5737:	            hmi_menu_logic_update_current_focus_menu_ptr();
 5738:	            fl_menu_cntrl_ptr = hmi_menu_logic_get_required_menu_instance(fl_menu_inst_no_U8);
 5739:	            hmi_menu_logic_Focusline_Center(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 5740:	            hmi_lsh_freeze_button_events_ext(fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_screen_id_U8,fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_prev_menu_id_E);
 5741:	
 5742:	      #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5743:	            /* Trigger LEFT WIPER*/
 5744:	            if(fl_cb_result_e != MENU_CB_ACTION_WAIT)
 5745:	            {
 5746:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_LEFT_ANM;
 5747:	                #ifdef HMI_MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION
 5748:	                    MENU_DO_FRAME_EXPAND_ANIMATION;
 5749:	                #endif
 5750:	            }
 5751:	            else
 5752:	            {
 5753:	                fl_cb_result_e = MENU_CB_ACTION_WIPER_LEFT_WAIT_ANM;
 5754:	            }
 5755:	      #endif
 5756:	
 5757:	
 5758:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_line_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_line_U16;
 5759:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_menu_id_E     = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id;
 5760:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_focus_entry_U16 = fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->focus_entry_U16;
 5761:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8   = hmi_menu_logic_get_screen_id(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr);
 5762:	
 5763:	        #ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5764:	            #ifdef HMI_MENU_CORE_CONTENT_MENU_ENABLED
 5765:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_REMOVE;
 5766:	            (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 5767:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 5768:	            (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 5769:	            #else
 5770:	            fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_screen_action_U8 = HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD;
 5771:	            (void)hmi_menu_logic_CB_result_anim(&fl_menu_anim_data, fl_cb_result_e);
 5772:	            #endif  
 5773:	        #else
 5774:	            if(MENU_CB_ACTION_EXIT == fl_cb_result_e)
 5775:	            {
 5776:	                hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Close(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id);
 5777:	            }
 5778:	            else
 5779:	            {
 5780:	                hmi_menu_logic_View_Mngr_Rq(fl_menu_cntrl_ptr->menu_id, fl_menu_anim_data.menu_anim_curr_screen_id_U8
 5781:	#ifdef LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED
 5782:	                    , ANIM_MAX_ID
 5783:	#endif
 5784:	                    , HMI_MENU_SCREEN_ADD);
 5785:	            }
 5786:	        #endif
 5787:	        }
 5788:	    }
 5789:	}
 5790:	
 5791:	/*============================================================================
 5792:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_parent_menu_id
 5793:	** Visibility:       global
 5794:	** Description:      Interface to get the parent menu id for the given menu id. If no parent, TOTAL_MENU_ID 
 5795:	**                   will be returned
 5796:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 5797:	** Inputs:           MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_curr_menu_id_U8
 5798:	** Outputs:          MENU_LIST_ID_T
 5799:	** Critical Section: None.
 5800:	** Created:          09-July-2014 by CSAKTHIV
 5801:	** Updated:
 5802:	**==========================================================================*/
 5803:	MENU_LIST_ID_T hmi_menu_logic_get_parent_menu_id(MENU_LIST_ID_T fl_curr_menu_id_U8)
 5804:	{
 5805:	    MENU_DEF_T  const   *fl_parent_menu_def_ptr;
 5806:	    MENU_LIST_ID_T       fl_parent_menu_id_e = TOTAL_MENU_ID;
 5807:	
 5808:	    fl_parent_menu_def_ptr  = hmi_menu_const_struct_array_table[fl_curr_menu_id_U8].parent_menu;
 5809:	    if(fl_parent_menu_def_ptr != NULL)
 5810:	    {
 5811:	        fl_parent_menu_id_e = hmi_menu_logic_get_menu_index(fl_parent_menu_def_ptr);
 5812:	    }
 5813:	    return fl_parent_menu_id_e;
 5814:	}
 5815:	#if ((defined (HMI_MENU_CORE_FOCUS_DISABLED_FOR_GREYOUT)&& defined(HMI_MENU_CORE_PAGE_BASED_FOCUS_FOR_GREYOUT)) || defined(LSH_ANIMATION_SUPPORT_ENABLED))
 5816:	/*============================================================================
 5817:	** Function Name:    hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number
 5818:	** Visibility:       global
 5819:	** Description:      Interface to get the page number of the focus entry based on the number of displayed lines.
 5820:	** Invocation:       Invoked by hmi_menu_logic.c
 5821:	** Inputs:           UINT16 fl_focus_entry_U16, UINT8 fl_displayed_lines_U8
 5822:	** Outputs:          
 5823:	** Critical Section: None.
 5824:	** Created:          23-August-2014 by CSAKTHIV
 5825:	** Updated:
 5826:	**==========================================================================*/
 5827:	static UINT16 hmi_menu_logic_get_page_number(UINT16 fl_focus_entry_U16, UINT8 fl_displayed_lines_U8)
 5828:	{
 5829:	    UINT16 fl_menu_page_number_U16;
 5830:	    if((fl_focus_entry_U16 == 0)||(fl_displayed_lines_U8 == 0))
 5831:	    {
 5832:	        fl_menu_page_number_U16 = 0;
 5833:	    }
 5834:	    else
 5835:	    {
 5836:	        fl_menu_page_number_U16 = (fl_focus_entry_U16/fl_displayed_lines_U8);
 5837:	    }
 5838:	    return fl_menu_page_number_U16;
 5839:	}
 5840:	#endif

 5942:	Traceability      : Menu Defect fixes
 5943:	Change Description: Made a FNULL check befor calling the callback().
 5944:	                    Button CB result set as MENU_PROCESS_MENU as default
 5945:	                    inside the hmi_menu_logic_button_processing()

 6704:	Traceability      :
 6705:	Change Description:  Task 284549: CGEA MFD: Right/Ok HK on Ipod in device list does
 6706:	                     not open track list
 6707:	                     Fix: hmi_menu_logic_LEFT_action, hmi_menu_logic_RIGHT_action and 
 6708:	           hmi_menu_logic_OK_action functions are modified to find absolute index for
 6709:	           dynamic menu configuration.
 6710:	
 6711:	                     Task 294375: Timer not restarting for two menu of the same instance
 6712:	                     Fix: Validation code added i hmi_menu_logic_Menu_Open to avoid 
 6713:	           stopping the timer when we open menu with the same instance.
 6714:	                     Root cause for this issue: When we open the menu with the same
 6715:	           instance as previous, 'hmi_menu_logic_disable_open_timer_status' function will
 6716:	           update 'timer_status_BOOL' value to false. In 'hmi_menu_logic_button_processing' 
 6717:	           menu core will start the timer only when 'timer_status_BOOL' is TRUE. So After 
 6718:	           the timer is getting elapsed, the menu is closed even when we do Up/Down action.
 6719:	                      
 6720:	                     'hmi_menu_logic_create_menu_instance'  function modified to destroy the
 6721:	           instance if we open the menu with the same instance.
 6722:	
 6723:	                     Use case :  
 6724:	                     Steps:
 6725:	                      Open M1 with instance 0.
 6726:	                      Open M2 with same instance in M1 close callback function.
 6727:	                      Do the same steps for > n times. n - maximum number of instance
 6728:	                     Issue - Menu will not be opened as l_nb_of_active_instance_S8 is
 6729:	                      exceeds the maximum number of instance
 6730:	                     Root cause:  In hmi_menu_logic_create_menu_instance, we are not doing  
 6731:	           any validation to increment the l_nb_of_active_instance_S8 value. So when we do  
 6732:	           this operation more than the no. of instance times, l_nb_of_active_instance_S8
 6733:	           incremented to the maximum value. After that if we open any high priority menu, 
 6734:	           it will not be opened as l_nb_of_active_instance_S8 exceeds the max no. of
 6735:	           instances. This is due to failure of destroying the current instance in
 6736:	           hmi_menu_logic_destroy_menu_instance function(hmi_menu_logic_destroy_menu_instance
 6737:	           is called in hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_close after the callback).           
 6738:	 
 6739:	-----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/

 6741:	Date              :  03-Mar-15

 6744:	Change Description:  1. MENU_CB_RESULT_T type changed from UINT8 to UINT16
 6745:	                     2. hmi_menu_logic_process_menu_action function return type changed
 6746:	              from UINT8 to MENU_CB_RESULT_T
 6747:	                     3. Updated HMI_MENU_CB_CLOSE_ANIM_MASK  value to (0x00FF) from Ox7F.
 6748:	              This macro is to extract the animation id from the menu ID. In the previous
 6749:	              version, animation exit condition is saved in 8th Bit of the menu id.This
 6750:	              will not work if the number of animation id is more than 127.

 6752:	              as UINT8 value instead of MENU_CB_RESULT

 6754:	              animation id to menu_close
